## Document 119: 261025_gs_TW-PCB-CCL

Source PDF: all report pdf/261025_gs_TW-PCB-CCL.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-25
Pages: 17
Extraction status: ok

### Page 2

PCB CCL upgrading continues: M10 is under qualification, which should start 
shipments from 2H27, while we expect M11 shipments to start no later than 2030  
For the PCB CCL industry overall, the key revenue/profit driver in our view is the solid 
specification upgrading trend (e.g., AI M7 ramp up in 2024, the M8 ramp in 2025, and 
M9 in 2026, Exhibit 3) which continues to drive high-end CCL players’ ASP growth by 
25-50% YoY annually in 2024-2026, and we are seeing the trend continuing and 
accelerating in coming years, despite the concerns of some investors in our 
conversations that a technology migration slowdown is impacting CCL players’ 
revenue/profit growth opportunity.  
During the TPCA show, we saw both EMC and TUC launch their M10/M11 solutions 
at the event, while both companies mentioned they are closely engaging with end 
customers for the next-generation AI R&D projects, based on the M10/11 solution (+ 
under qualification now). We believe EMC should start shipping its M10 products from 
2H27 at the earliest for specific AI customer’s GPU AI rack solutions and that shipments 
should ramp up significantly in 2028 as more GPU and ASIC AI projects start adopting 
this solution. For TUC’s M10 products, we believe the company will start shipment 
from 2028 for not only high-end 3.2T Ethernet switch projects but also for some ASIC 
projects.  
Both EMC and TUC are currently under verification with clients for their M10/M11 grade 
CCL, which incorporates Q-glass. In terms of the possibility of adopting PTFE-based 
CCL, the new solution continues to face production difficulties, making us less 
positive on the solution’s progress, and we only expect the MP (mass production) 
time to occur after 2H28 at the earliest (our supply chain checks suggest the 
production yield for PTFE-based PCB is running at 10-15% vs. average yield of 
80-90% for PCB with M9 grade CCL adopting Q-glass). As a result, we believe the PCB 
upgrade for switch tray in Nvidia’s Rubin Ultra GPU servers starting from 2H27 could 
have a high likelihood of adopting M9/M10 grade CCL with Q-glass/HC (Hydrocarbon) 
instead of PTFE, potentially implying better EMC/TUC share than we currently expect 
over the next 2-3 years).

### Page 3

New opportunity to enter the high ASP/GM ABF substrate supply chain for TW CCL 
supply chain players 
Given the relatively slow capacity investment to date from Japanese substrate material 
suppliers (ABF CCL/ T-glass/ micro thin copper foil etc.industries are mostly dominated 
by Japanese players, with more than 95% market share in the past 10+ years) while 
demand continues to trend up (we are seeing ABF substrates’ material usage doubling 
every 1-2 years, due to the expanding substrate size/ increasing substrate layer counts / 
decreasing substrate production yield rate), we started to see end customers 
qualification processes for Taiwan and mainland China ABF substrate supply chain 
vendors accelerate from 1H26, to better overcome supply constraints, implying a 
long term share gain trend from the Taiwan/mainland China new substrate material 
suppliers (including EMC and Co-Tech in our coverage space). We therefore believe 
EMC likely starts taking ABF substrate CCL market share from 2H26 (TAM could be 
up from US$0.8bn-1.2bn in 2026 to US$2.0bn-2.5bn in 2027 and US$4.0bn-4.5bn in

### Page 4

2028, accounting for ~15%/19%/22% of EMC’s 2026/27/28E revenue, per GSe, 
which not only suggests significant upside potential for EMC’s ASP outlook (ABF 
substrate CCL ASP per full sheet is ~US$1,500 vs. M7/8 only at US$130/180, Exhibit 
5) but should also lead to a solid GM uptrend. We estimate the ABF substrate CCL GM is 
now at 50%+ which could further increase closer to the industry average of ~70%+ 
in the long term based on our industry checks (vs. our 2026/27/28 GM estimate on 
EMC is 35.2%/40.7%/43.4%). Also, we believe that micro-thin copper foil, which is 
dominated by Japan vendors, should also start to see some new suppliers starting from 
late 2027/early 2028, while Co-Tech said that it is preparing R&D resources and is 
working on designs for the new production lines for this new substrate-level product, 
which we estimate could start contributing to the company’s revenue from 2H28 at the 
earliest per GSe.  
Moreover, considering that the ABF substrate size/layer count both continue to increase, 
with less favorable yield rate and production efficiency, we expect the high-end AI ABF 
substrates consumption on ABF CCL to increase significantly. Based on prevailing 
specifications, we believe a quarter sheet of ABF CCL can currently produce ~6-8 units 
of high-end ABF substrates, but that number could decrease significantly to 2-3 units 
per quarter sheet in 2028 based on the substrate design, which should lead to a solid 
unit volume demand uptrend.  
In terms of supply, EMC said that it could have 1.5mn sheets/month capacity by end of 
2027 for ABF substrate CCL during TPCA event, which would then likely account for 50% 
of the global ABF substrate CCL capacity on our estimates, suggesting a strong focus by 
the company to compete in this high ASP/GM product segment.

PCB/CCL upgrades to continue with limited risk from CPO adoption 
We see limited near term risk of Co-Packaged Optics (CPO) adoption as the industry 
should start with switch trays in GPU servers from 2028E, when we estimate switch 
board to account for 14% of a total CCL TAM of US$48bn in 2028E. In considering an 
extreme case where all switch boards are downgraded to M4/M7 grade CCL, we would 
expect the impact to result in a <10% reduction in the 2028E CCL TAM, an impact which 
we believe would be manageable. At the TPCA show, we saw ZDT showcase its 30 layers

### Page 5

HDI PCB switch board, and the company said that it is confident that switch board will
continue to see upgrades in terms of layer count and design complexity as optical
transceivers are being used as the mainstream solution for scale out, which requires high
end PCB/CCL for its longer electrical path comparing to CPO (Exhibit 7). ZDT mentioned
the possibility of scale up adopting CPO, but is unsure on the MP timing at this point, and
we see this technology as unlikely to be available before 2030E given its technology

complexities.
ZDT also showcased multiple optical module mSAP solutions, including
800G/1.6T/NPO/XPO, and we believe that optical transceivers, which rely on high end
PCB/CCL in switch trays, will remain mainstream in the foreseeable future and thus is
beneficial to the optical module mSAP suppliers (we expect optical module mSAP to see
87% 2026-28E CAGR, Exhibit 8). We believe ZDT is well-positioned to gain share in this
fast growing market as we believe the company is proactively expanding mSAP capacity

(we expect ZDT’s mSAP capacity to reach 5x in the next 2 years, see here, as well as the
company currently holds an industry-leading yield of 90%+ for its optical module mSAP
products. Optical module mSAP GMs currently average 40%-45%, and could potentially
reach 50%+ following pricing hikes and yield improvements, which would be beneficial
to ZDT’s profitability (our 2026/27/28 GM estimate on ZDT only at
24.7%/27.6%/31.4%).

gainers
EMC to see solid upside on profit potential with the new substrate CCL opportunity
We continue to see EMC make progress in the new ABF substrate CCL business, a market
which the company entered in early 2026 and started producing high-end ABF substrate
CCL from 2H26, with monthly revenues reaching NT$150-200mn (<1% of the company’s
Sep monthly revenue) for the most high-end TPU substrate CCL market (see here). We
believe EMC could become one of the market leaders within the next 3-5 years (we are

expecting 20-30% market share in 2028E and trending up significantly afterward), given
its track record of successfully entering new markets over the past two decades.
Furthermore, the company is well-positioned to gain share in the tight supply market as
the company is proactive with its substrate CCL expansion plans, and the company
expects to expand its substrate CCL capacity to 4-5x of current level by 2027 (current
substrate CCL capacity at 300k sheet/month), which should account for 50% of the

global substrate CCL capacity by end of 2027E, implying a good opportunity for the
company to further ramp up market share in the long term.
M10/11 upgrades still the key to earnings growth for EMC & TUC in the next 3+
years, while the favorable S/D outlook should continue to drive the like-for-like
base pricing in coming quarters
We see EMC and TUC being well-positioned in the ongoing CCL upgrade trend as both
companies are currently under verification with clients for their respective next

generation M10/M11 grade CCL and are also future proofing their product-portfolios by
continuing to develop PTFE-based CCL. For the new M10 grade CCL, we expect EMC will
start shipments from 2Q27-3Q27 while TUC should start shipments from 2028, leading
to meaningful upside potential for each company’s revenue, as EMC/TUC’s current ASPs
have been approximately US$63/46 in 2026 while the M10 grade CCL ASP is currently
priced at ~US$800 per sheet, implying substantial ASP upside potential from here, all

else equal.
We continue to see a solid S/D outlook for high-end CCLs in coming years (see here) as
capacity expansion for these CCLs continues to track slower than demand growth (see
here). However, we note TUC revised up its capacity target and expects it to increase
2.5x+ over 2026-30 (8/5/30% upward revision to the year-end target capacity for
2026/27/28 from previous guidance), and based on company comments we believe the
new capacity is fully booked by clients, implying a solid revenue/profit growth outlook.

Co-Tech is currently obtaining LTAs to secure high-end copper foil capacity,
suggesting a solid long term market share and revenue outlook
We believe Co-Tech is well-positioned to benefit from the structural demand for high
end copper foil in a shortage environment, as the company announced capacity
expansion plans for a new Plant 4 in Malaysia, with capacity being secured by more than
one client, per the company. The company now target its annual HVLP3+ effective

capacity to reach 10K/20K/30K tons by end of 2027/28/29 (equivalent of
833/1666/2500 tons/month by 2027/28/29 end vs. current HVLP3+ effective capacity
of ~250 tons/month). This should enable the company to achieve further gains in market
share amid high end copper foil supply shortage environment (see here), as well as
seeing a better pricing environment going forward.
We expect Co-Tech’s new capacity, after ramp up, should continue to see 100% UTR, as

### Page 8

most of the new capacity has been booked/sponsored by end customers already, 
implying a solid market share and revenue outlook in coming years. Moreover, we believe 
the company will start to recognize customer subsidies from 2027 at the earliest, 
implying further incremental upside to profitability in coming years.  
ZDT continues to maintain key one-stop shop status for all ABF/BT/HDI/HLC/mSAP 
products, suggesting solid market share outlook in long term 
We remain positive on ZDT as the company continues to make progress with its optical 
module mSAP and AI PCB business, and the company mentioned that both of these 
business are seeing capacity bookings by clients. We believe ZDT will become a market 
share leader in the optical module mSAP industry (see here), as we expect the company 
to expand mSAP capacity by 5x over the next two years based on announced capex 
plans. Most suppliers in the optical module mSAP industry are proactively expanding 
capacity as CPO adoption risk remains limited.  
Also, considering that ZDT is the only company that manufactures all types of 
ABF/BT/HDI/HLC/mSAP products, it is well positioned for further share gains not only in 
the optical transceiver market, but also in the AI PCB, ABF, BT markets. Additionally, we 
estimate that these new products will likely carry 35-50%+ GMs in 2027, implying a solid 
revenue/profit outlook for the company going forward.  
We also see other companies within the CCL/IC substrate sector flagging supply 
tightness across the whole supply chain as industry expansion has not been sufficient to 
meet prevailing demand. This is in line with our view that suppliers with timely capacity 
expansion plans are better positioned to make further market share gains and benefit 
from stronger pricing/profitability amid a supply-constrained environment.

### Page 10

Investment Thesis - Elite Material  
EMC, a key supplier of high-end HDI material (70%+ market share) and SLP material 
(90%+ market share), has been focusing on the high-speed switch/server CCL market for 
3-5+ years. We are positive on its market share trajectory in the server industry and 
expect EMC to increase its share from <10% in the Purley generation to 15-20%/20+% 
in the Whitley/Eagle Stream platforms. EMC is also proactively expanding its share in the 
switch market (40%/30%+ in 2024/23 vs. <5% before 2019), with an expanding AI 
customer base (only one 400G customer in 3Q20, to being one of the largest suppliers in 
2023), which should continue to benefit the company’s top/bottom line growth over 
time. We believe EMC’s leading position in the AI server CCL market should drive strong 
revenue growth and GM/OPM expansion in the long term, given that it is the major 
supplier for all Nvidia/Google/AWS AI server projects, and we expect it to maintain its 
leadership position. Considering its leading position in the high-end CCL industry, solid 
production skills and earnings growth outlook, we are Buy rated on the stock. With the 
shares trading at a 2026E P/E below AI server component suppliers and supported by a 
solid growth outlook, we view valuation as attractive. 
Key downside risks include: (1) RCC to replace all high-end smartphone HDI design; (2) 
rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker smartphone and server shipments; and 
(3) rising competition from mainland China peers.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Elite Material  
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$10,200 is based on 27x 2H27-1H28E P/E 
(in line with the peak P/E multiple for AI component suppliers in the past 3 years).  
Key downside risks:  (1) RCC to replace all high-end smartphone HDI design; (2) rising 
trade tensions, which could lead to weaker smartphone and server shipments; and (3) 
rising competition from mainland China peers.

### Page 11

Investment Thesis - Taiwan Union Technology Corp. 
TUC is a key high-end global CCL supplier that focuses on M7+ grade high-speed CCL 
(key applications include high-end switches (100G+) and AI server materials), with 20%+ 
market share in the past two years. The company continues to expect its unit market 
share in the server industry to increase from 15% in Whitley and Purley processors to 
higher in the Eagle Stream generation, and plans to launch two types of products for 
low-end and high-end customers (T2A and T2C) to gain market share. Also, strong 
growth in 400G/800G switch shipments and new 800G switch shipments, scheduled for 
launch in 2025-27, should continue to be a key demand driver for TUC, in our view. 
Additionally, the company is working on high-end AI projects with CSP and enterprise 
players, which we believe should drive revenue momentum in the long term. We view the 
stock as undervalued vs. Taiwan CCL peers on a P/E basis and rate it Buy.  
Key downside risks include: (1) slower-than-expected share gains in the low-loss CCL 
segment; (2) rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker server and switch 
shipments globally; and (3) rising competition from mainland China peers.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Taiwan Union Technology Corp. 
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$3,010 is based on a 22x 2027E P/E (+2x 
STDV higher than the past 3-year industry average P/E multiple).  
Key downside risks:  (1) slower-than-expected share gains in the low-loss CCL segment; 
(2) rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker server and switch shipments 
globally; and (3) rising competition from mainland China peers. 
Investment Thesis - Co-Tech 
Co-Tech is a global leading general server copper foil supplier, with ~60% market share 
in 2025 per GSe. We expect the company to enjoy solid share gain opportunities in the 
near term, considering its proactive HVLP capacity expansion plan and strong 
know-how in the advanced copper foil industry. Also, adoption of HVLP4 copper foil 
from AI servers and 1.6T networking switches starting in 2H26 should be a key demand 
driver for Co-Tech, in our view. Additionally, the company’s proactive pricing strategy 
and ability to improve production yield should drive revenue momentum in the long 
term.  
We are Buy rated as we believe Co-Tech will see better revenue/profitability in the long 
term given its shift to a more premium product mix along with proactive capacity plans 
(Plant 3 to be ready by end of 2027) and this should lead to strengthened valuations.

Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$940 is based on a 22x 2028E P/E, which is 
+1SD above the company’s average upcycle valuation in the past 10-years. 
Key downside risks: (1) earlier than expected CPO adoption in the computing IC, (2) 
slower than expected yield rate improvement speed, and (3) much stronger than 
expected capacity growth from HVLP peers, limiting pricing power

### Page 12

Investment Thesis - Zhen Ding Technology
Zhen Ding Technology (ZDT) is the global leading PCB supplier (top 1 in market share in
sales) with 7-10% market share, and started its ABF substrate business from 2023 in
Shenzhen, China; it has the highest ABF revenue exposure to the China market compared
to all other ABF substrate suppliers. We believe ZDT will hold more than 40% ABF
substrate market share in China by 2027E (up from 31% in 2024) and benefit the most

from the strong and growing China ABF substrate market (53% 2025-27E CAGR) due to
the increasing in-sourcing ratio for the AI computing ASIC/GPU market in China. We also
expect the increasing foldable smartphone penetration rate in the long term to drive
ZDT’s FPC TAM, and account for 39% of the company’s total revenue in 2027E. We see
further revenue upside potential from foldable phones. We are Buy rated mainly due to
our positive view on the company’s China ABF substrate business opportunity, which

should not only drive ZDT’s revenue growth, but also improve its OPM level in the long
term. We see upside for the stock price considering the company’s solid growth
opportunity, and our TP implied P/E multiple is still lower than the company’s past 10+
year P/E average, which we believe is fair considering the potential upside from China
ABF substrate market and increasing foldable phone penetration rate.
Valuation: Our 12-month TP of NT$1,025 is based on a SOTP valuation methodology,

including (1) 3.5x blended 2028E P/B for ABF & BT substrate, (2) 20x 2028E P/E for
high-growth AI server/switch related PCB (in line with industry average), and (3) 8x
2028E P/E for low-growth consumer electronics PCB (in line with industry downcycle
average valuation).
Key downside risks include: (1) weaker-than-expected iPhone demand or slower
content upgrade, (2) delay in new substrate capacity or slower-than-expected yield rate
ramp up progress, and (3) fiercer competition in China ABF market.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

OSAT 展望樂觀，SOIC 機台具技術優勢 
 2027 主要成長動能仍為 OSAT:2026 年弘塑產能擴充一倍 ，1Q26 已
經完成擴建 ，3Q26 新產能已經滿載。 外包部分 ， 目前外包比例33%，
未來佔比會下降，目前外包月產能一個月 5-6 台，因為外包產能受
限，同業也在搶外包產能。2027 主要成長動能仍為 OSAT，今年晶
圓代工客戶營收高於 OSAT，2027 年 OSAT 營收超過晶圓代工客戶，
SOIC 機台去年小量，今年量增，2027 跟 2026 差不多略增，2028
才會明顯大增，主要還是跟隨晶圓代工客戶 SOIC 擴廠進度。 
 2028-2029 年 PLP 機台出貨量會很明顯放大:PLP 及 CoPoS 設備機
台單價很高，初估有機會是 CoWoS 翻倍，PLP 進度比較快，PLP
規格弘塑 310*310、510*515、600*600 都有，再大到 700 以上就不
做了，晶圓代工客戶規格是 310*310 但目前進度比較慢，還在實驗
線階段。力成 510*515 進度最快，4Q26 會拉第一批機台，2027 隨
力成購置的友達廠進度持續拉機台，2028-2029 年 PLP 機台出貨量
會很明顯放大。 
 維持買進評等，目標價 3000 元：基本面無變化，預估數字也沒做調
整，故維持買進評等 ，預估 2026 年 EPS 64.81 元，2027 年 EPS 93.97
元，目標價 3000 元。(2027 PER 30x)

2Q26 毛 利率 39.37%， 低於預
期，2Q26 EPS 11.18 元 
 2Q26 毛利率 39.37%，低於預期，2Q26 EPS 11.18 元 
弘塑 2Q26 EPS 11.18 元，低於元富預估的 12.34 元，獲利達成率 91%。本業
來看，2Q26 營收 17.27 億元，+8%QOQ，+6%YOY，2Q26 毛利率 39.37%，
較 1Q26 回升 5.1 個百分點，然仍低於預估的 41%，主要是外包成本及急單增
加影響部分毛利率，預期 3Q26 毛利率持續向上改善，2Q26 EPS 11.18 元。

弘塑 3Q26 營收 22.67 億元，+31.3%QOQ，+51.8%YOY，營收達成率 98%，
略低於預期，預估 3Q26 毛利率 41%，預估 3Q26 EPS 16.47 元。

3Q26 自結稅前淨利年增 27%，優於預期 7%  
鈊象 3Q26 營收 67.5 億元(季增 5.2%/年增 25.3%)，自結營業利益率季增 1.9
個百分點，反映高毛利授權收入佔比提升及營收規模擴大，帶動稅前淨利
42.9 億元(季增 8.6%/年增 27.4%)，優於我們/市場預期 7%/5%，推估 EPS 
12.55 元。展望 4Q26，9 月單月授權收入提升至 17.5 億元(vs 2025 年底 14
億元)，隨各國業務推進，估遊戲授權營收年增 33%，帶動整體營收年增
19.9%至 68.3 億元，預估 EPS 12.56 元(季持平/年增 18%)，上修 4%。 
美國跨州加速推進，估 2026/27 年遊戲授權年增 32%/15% 
網路遊戲授權為鈊象主要獲利成長引擎，預期 2026/27 年授權收入年增
32%/15%。以 1-8M26 區域別而言，1) 美國營收年增 65%、占比 14%，目
前全美共 35 個州可合法經營 Sweepstakes 相關業務，鈊象於深耕之三大州
市占率約 60%，過去三年營收表現平穩，近期透過新型 Social SS 拓展至其
他 32 州，將為未來數年最主要成長動能； 2) 東南亞營收年增 18%、占比
54%，雖基期已高，但考量市佔率低且部分地區仍有新增合作機會，預期維
持穩健成長；3) 其他地區營收年增 86%、占比 11%，南美洲近一年表現亮
眼、非洲自去年底進入市場後亦逐步取得進展、歐洲雖已取得多國執照且營
收持續成長，惟受國際大廠競爭、品牌知名度影響拓展速度相對較慢。 
海外授權多點開花，維持買進評等 
看好隨美國業務跨州加速推進、東南亞市場穩健成長，及 TaDa Gaming 品
牌在歐洲及其他新興市場持續擴張，帶動鈊象 2026/27 年營收年增 19%/10%；
隨高毛利遊戲授權業務佔比提升至 76%/79%、營運槓桿發酵，估營業利益
率年增 1.1 個/1.5 個百分點，EPS 年增 24.3%/11.5%至 47.88 元/53.38 元，
上修 3%/1%，評價維持歷史區間中值 20 倍本益比，以 2H26-1H27 EPS 
50.85 元(前次為 2026 年)，推得目標價 1,000 元，給予買進。 
元大觀點 
⧫  
3Q26 遊戲授權營收年增 33%，佔比提升至 77%，使自結稅前淨利年增
27%，優於預期 7%；4Q26 授權收入持續疊加，估營收年增 20%。 
⧫  
美國跨州快速推進成為主要動能、東南亞穩健成長、TaDa Gaming 在
歐洲及新興市場持續擴張，估 2026/27 年授權收入年增 32%/15%。 
⧫  
看好鈊象高毛利授權占比提升、營運槓桿發酵，以 20 倍本益比及
2H26-1H27 EPS 50.85 元，推得目標價 1,000 元，維持買進評等。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：軟體 2026 年 10 月 22 日 IGS 第2 頁，共 15頁  
營運分析  
3Q26 網路遊戲授權營收年增 43%，自結稅前淨利年增 27%，優於預期 7% 
鈊象 3Q26 營收 67.5 億元(季增 5.2%/年增 25.3%)，達成我們/市場預期 103%/101%，略優於預期，
營收佔比為網路遊戲授權 77%、網路遊戲 APP 21% 、商用遊戲機 2%，暑假遊戲旺季，且世界盃
投注熱潮外溢至其他線上博弈項目，帶動網路遊戲授權營收季增 8%/年增 43%；自結營業利益 41.6
億元(季增 8.5%/年增 34.3%)，營業利益率季增 1.9 個百分點至 61.7%，優於我們預期 1.8 個百分
點，反映高毛利授權收入佔比提升及營收規模擴大，自結稅前淨利 42.9 億元(季增 8.6%/年增
27.4%)，優於我們/市場預期 7%/5%，推估 EPS 12.55 元。 
三大業務 1-9M26 狀況如下： 
➢ 網路遊戲授權(營收佔比 77%)：累計營收年增 32%，美國及其他地區成長快速、東南亞穩健
擴張，單月遊戲授權收入提升至 17-18 億元，相對 2025 年底約 14 億元成長。 
➢ 網路遊戲 APP (21%) ：累計營收年減 6%，台灣市場已接近飽和及競爭激烈，且觀察 APP 玩
家付費率(ARPU)近年來維持在 1.5%至 2%之間，顯示休閒玩家的付費意願已達極限。 
➢ 商用遊戲機(2%)：累計營收年減 26%，營收佔比下滑，中國內需疲弱、競爭者新品推出快速，
且部分遊樂場停止經營使中古機台流入市場，壓抑新機需求。

授權收入持續疊加，估 4Q26 營收年增 20% 
9 月底鈊象單月遊戲授權收入提升至逾 17.5 億元(vs 2025 年底 14 億元)，隨公司於各國業務持續推
進，授權收入有望進一步擴張，估 4Q26 網路遊戲授權營收年增 33%至 53 億元，帶動整體營收年
增 19.9%至 68.3 億元，上修 2%，預估營業利益 42.1 億元(季增 1.1%/年增 24.5%)、稅後淨利 35.4
億元(季持平/年增 18%)，估 EPS 12.56 元，上修 4%。

海外版圖持續擴張，估 2026/27 年網路遊戲授權營收年增 32%/15% 
網路遊戲授權為鈊象主要獲利成長引擎，1-9M26 網路遊戲授權營收 145 億元，年增 32%，營收占
比自 2025 年之 68%提升至 75%，於遊戲授權業務毛利率達 100%，進一步推動公司毛利率提升。 
以 1-8M26 區域別而言， 1) 美國營收年增 65%、占比 14%，目前全美共  35 個州可合法經營
Sweepstakes 相關業務，鈊象於深耕之三大州市占率約 60%，過去三年營收表現平穩，近期透過新
型 Social SS 拓展至其他 32 州，將為未來數年最主要成長動能，同時我們認為於美國更長遠成長
引擎，將來自跨足市場規模數倍於 SS 領域的 online casino 市場；2) 東南亞營收年增 18%、占比
維持54%，雖基期已高，但考量市佔率低且部分地區仍有新增合作機會，預期維持穩健成長； 3) 其
他地區(排除台灣、中國、美國、東南亞)營收年增 86%、占比 11%，以 TaDa Gaming 品牌業務為
主，其中南美洲近一年表現亮眼、非洲自去年底進入市場後亦逐步取得進展、歐洲為全球最大線上
博弈市場，雖已取得多國執照且營收持續成長，惟受國際大廠競爭、品牌知名度影響拓展速度相對
較慢，公司正積極擴大代理商合作以加速產品上架排程。 
整體而言，隨美國業務跨州加速推進、東南亞市場穩健貢獻，及 TaDa Gaming 品牌在歐洲及其他
新興市場持續擴張，預期鈊象 2026/27 年網路授權遊戲收入 198 億/229 億元，年增 32%/15%。

遊戲授權業務多點開花，估 2026/27 年 EPS 年增 24%/11%  
展望 2026/27 年，預估鈊象營收年增 19%/10%至 263 億/290 億元，主要動能來自美國業務跨州加
速推進、東南亞市場穩健貢獻，及 TaDa Gaming 品牌在歐洲及其他新興市場持續擴張，估帶動網
路遊戲授權營收年增 32%/15%、網路遊戲 app 則因台灣市場已接近飽和及競爭激烈、付費率見頂，
預估營收年減 7%/5%、商用遊戲機受中國消費力衰退及美國關稅不利因素影響，預估營收年減
28%/年持平；隨高毛利遊戲授權業務佔比提升至 76%/79%、營運槓桿發酵，預估帶動營業利益率
提升年增 1.1 個/1.5 個百分點至 60.4%/61.9%，使營業利益年增 21%/13%至 159 億/179 億元、稅
後淨利年增 24%/12%至 134 億/150 億元，估 EPS 47.88 元/53.38 元，上修 3%/1%。

### Page 5

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：軟體 2026 年 10 月 22 日 IGS 第5 頁，共 15頁  
產業概況  
全球線上博弈市場規模將以 CAGR 10.5%成長至 2034 年 2,864 億美元 
根據 Facts and Factors 2026/2月報告， 2025 年全球線上博弈市場規模約 1,055 億美元，預計將以
CAGR 10.5% 擴張至 2034 年達 2,864 億美元，動能包括多國政府積極推動線上博弈合法化，以爭
取稅收增長、行動裝置及 5G 網路普及、數位支付機制成熟，以及 AI 技術在玩家行為分析與精準行
銷上的廣泛應用。按照遊戲類型劃分，體育博弈佔比最高達 48%、其次為線上賭場；按照區域劃分，
歐洲佔 41%為最大的線上博弈市場，歸因於多國具備極為完善且透明的監管體系、北美多州逐步開
放線上博弈，帶動市場成長、拉丁美洲與雅泰地區因基期低及滲透率提升，預期將快速成長。  
圖 7：2021-2034 年全球線上博弈市場規模預測

資料來源：Global market insight、元大投顧 
社交博弈不涉及現金提現，純娛樂性質降低法規監管門檻 
社交博弈不同於傳統線上博弈，其核心在於不涉及現金提現的純娛樂性質，主要受網路遊戲法規監
管，根據 The Business Research Company《2026 年社交博弈全球市場報告》，2025 年全球社交
博弈市場規模為 92.4 億美元，預計將以 CAGR 9.1% 持續成長，至 2030 年達 144 億美元，動能來
自全球網路普及率提升進而擴大博弈遊戲的受眾，此外業者積極開發創新的技術平台與互動機制，
有效提升社交博弈市場的獲利。在區域表現方面，北美為全球最大社交博弈市場，亞太地區受惠於
人口紅利與網路設備普及，預期將成為成長最快速的區域。

### Page 6

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：軟體 2026 年 10 月 22 日 IGS 第6 頁，共 15頁  
全球遊戲市場 2025-2028 年 CAGR 達 4.9%，PC 遊戲成長最強勁 
根據 Newzoo 2025/12 遊戲產業市場報告，2025 年全球遊戲市場總收入預計達 1,967 億美元，年增 
7.5%，成長動能來自既有玩家更強的付費深度與更長的時間投入。細分平台市場，手機遊戲 (佔比
55%)年增 7.7%，受益於成熟的線上服務與廣大的全球覆蓋率、PC 遊戲(22%)年增 10.4%，受惠於
高品質遊戲的密集發行，以及 Steam 平台在亞洲市場滲透率提升、主機遊戲(23%)年增 4.2%，受
惠硬體設備更替及指標性作品推出。 
展望未來，預估全球遊戲產業 2025-2028 年將以 4.9%的 CAGR 成長至 2,272 億美元，並以 PC 遊
戲的 6.6%的 CAGR 表現最為強勁，其市場規模有望於 2028 年超越主機遊戲，主要受惠 Steam 降
低遊戲開發及供給門檻、東亞與新興市場玩家數量增加，以及於數位原生代 (Z 世代和 Alpha 世代)
成為消費主力。 
圖 9：2022-2028 年全球遊戲市場規模預測

資料來源：Newzoo、元大投顧 
台灣遊戲市場維持成長趨勢，但付費意願下滑 
根據資誠《2023-2027 全球娛樂暨媒體業展望》報告指出， 2024 年台灣電玩遊戲與電競產業產值
約達 39 億美元，2022 年至 2027 年 CAGR 達 4.8%，至 2027 年產值達 44.6 億美元，主要動能來
自 1) 智慧型手機滲透率高；2) 高速數據傳輸方案價格親民；3) 5G 覆蓋率持續提升等。在付費狀況
方面，台灣在全球行動遊戲營收中，每使用者平均收入 (ARPU)長期位居全球前十名，根據資策會
統計，台灣網友遊玩數位遊戲的滲透率已高達 81.4%，但受疫後消費回歸線下、通膨及影音社群等
多元娛樂排擠資源影響，2025 年整體玩家具付費習慣的比例下降至 35.5%，較前一年下滑約一成，
反應消費者對免費內容的依賴度提升，在付費決策上更趨於精打細算，未來市場的獲利動能在於透
過精準的數據分析與高黏著度的內容經營，深挖核心付費族群的長期價值。

獲利調整與股票評價  
遊戲授權業務多點開花，給予鈊象買進評等，目標價 1,000,元 
展望 2026/27 年，預估鈊象營收年增 19%/10%至 263 億/290 億元，主要動能來自美國業務跨州加
速推進、東南亞市場穩健貢獻，及 TaDa Gaming 品牌在歐洲及其他新興市場持續擴張，估帶動網
路遊戲授權營收年增 32%/15%，網路遊戲 app 則因台灣市場已接近飽和及競爭激烈、付費率見頂，
預估營收年減 7%/5%，商用遊戲機受中國消費力衰退及美國關稅不利因素影響，預估營收年減
28%/年持平；隨高毛利遊戲授權業務佔比提升至 76%/79%、營運槓桿發酵，預估帶動營業利益率
提升年增 1.1 個/1.5 個百分點至 60.4%/61.9%，使營業利益年增 21%/13%至 159 億/179 億元、稅
後淨利年增 24%/12%至 134 億/150 億元，估 EPS 47.88 元/53.38 元，上修 3%/1%。 
鈊象歷史三年交易於 10-30 倍本益比，看好公司海外遊戲授權業務保持擴張態勢，全球線上博弈市
場規模 2025 至 2034 年將以 CAGR 10.5% 成長，鈊象於既有市場擴大市佔，並持續開拓新市場，
帶動獲利逐年走升，此次評價維持歷史區間中值 20 倍本益比，以 2H26-1H27 EPS 50.85 元(前次
為 2026 年)，推得目標價 1,000 元，給予買進評等。

### Page 11

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：軟體 2026 年 10 月 22 日 IGS 第11 頁，共 15頁

公司簡介  
鈊象成立於 1989 年，為台灣商用遊戲機與網路遊戲大廠，專注於遊戲軟硬體之設計、研發、製造與行銷，初期以發展商用
遊戲機為主，擁有出色的 IP 如《三國戰記》和《西遊》等。然而為分散中國街機市場競爭和高收入集中風險，鈊象 2000 年
跨入 PC 與線上遊戲產業，推出經典麻將線上遊戲「明星三缺一」、「唯舞獨尊」及「封神 Online」等，為公司的社交娛樂
場產品組合奠定堅實的基礎。2010 年起在智慧手機崛下時空背景下，鈊象 2015 年線上遊戲業務比重正式超越商用遊戲機，
而近五年隨自營 APP 產品玩家人數逐漸飽和，鈊象發展重心轉往海外授權，與當地代理商合作收取授權金以及服務費。
2025 年營收比重為：網路授權遊戲 68%、網路遊戲 APP 29%、商用遊戲機 2%。

### Page 12

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：軟體 2026 年 10 月 22 日 IGS 第12 頁，共 15頁

ESG 分析  
分別藉由「在 ESG 議題上的曝險」和「個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力」兩個面向來衡量公司於 ESG 上的表現。 
► ESG 總分：鈊象整體的 ESG 風險評級屬於低風險，於 Sustainalytics 資料庫所覆蓋的公司中排行在略微領先的位置，於
娛樂軟體業的公司中排名落後同業。 
► 在 ESG 議題上的曝險：鈊象的整體曝險較低，略低於行業平均。人力資本、資料隱私與網路安全以及商業道德是值得關
注的重要 ESG 議題。 
► 個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力：鈊象在針對重大 ESG 議題的管理水準和執行力屬平均水準。該公司展現出非常強而
有力的環境政策和嚴格的 ESG 報告標準。其舉報人保護機制尚可完善，但仍有提升空間與保護機制的必要性。 ESG 治
理水準適中，設有 ESG 相關委員會，但其權限低於董事會。

主要負責撰寫本研究報告全文或部分內容之分析師，茲針對本報告所載證券或證券發行機構，於此聲明： (1) 文中所述觀點皆準確反映其個人對各證券或
證券發行機構之看法；(2) 研究部分析師於本研究報告中所提出之特定投資建議或觀點，與其過去、現在、未來薪酬的任何部份皆無直接或間接關聯。

投資評等說明 
買進：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持正面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中增持該股。 
持有-超越同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力高於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
持有-落後同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力低於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
賣出：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持負面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中減持該股。 
評估中：本中心之預估、評等、目標價尚在評估中，但仍積極追蹤該個股。 
限制評等：為遵循相關法令規章及/或元大之政策，暫不給予評等及目標價。

註：元大給予個股之目標價係依 12 個月投資期間計算。大中華探索系列報告並無正式之 12 個月目標價，其投資建議乃根據分析師報告中之指定期間分析
而得。

本報告僅提供一般資訊，文中所載資訊或任何意見，並不構成任何買賣證券或其他投資標的之要約或要約之引誘。報告資料之刊發僅供客戶一般傳閱用途，
並非意欲提供專屬之投資建議，亦無考慮任何可能收取本報告之人士的個別財務狀況與目標。對於投資本報告所討論或建議之任何證券、投資標的，或文
中所討論或建議之投資策略，投資人應就其是否適合本身而諮詢財務顧問的意見。本報告之內容取材自據信為可靠之資料來源，但概不以明示或默示的方

時碩 (4566  TT)  Trading Buy

2H26 營運明顯增長 ，投資建議 Trading Buy 。  
投 資 建 議 Trading Buy ： 雖 目 前 時碩 (4566 TT) 處於 CB 緘 默 期 ，
未 提 供 明 確 展 望 ， 但 受 惠 於 航 太 、 散 熱 產 品 出 貨 增 長 ，3Q26 營
運 已 明 顯 攀 升 。 考 量 2026 、 2027 年 營 運 動 能 向 上 ， 投 資 建 議
Trading Buy ， 目 標 價 92 元 (以 202 7 年 EPS 及 PE  17 倍 評 價 )。  
時 碩 積 極 布 局 高 成 長 潛 力 的 利 基 型 市 場 ， 包 含 液 冷 散 熱 系 統 、
人 形 機 器 人 零 組 件 ： 液冷散熱 方面， 時 碩 致 力 於 提 供 一 站 式 的
全 方 位 熱 管 理 解 決 方 案 ， 其 產 品 線 涵 蓋 快 接 頭 、 分 歧 管 、 冷 卻
液 分 配 單 元 、 微 通 道 板 及 技 術 門 檻 較 高 的 水 冷 板 ， 並 能 提 供 不
鏽 鋼 與 銅 等 多 樣 材 質 。公司 已 成 功 獲 得 美 、 日 系 液 冷 系 統 大 廠
訂單，且於 2H26 放量 出貨 ， 預估 散 熱 產 品 佔 202 6 年 營 收 比 重
有望 接近 10% 。 人 形 機 器 人方 面 ， 時 碩 積 極 拓 展 此 領 域 的 關 鍵
零 組 件 業 務 ， 產 品 涵 蓋 諧 波 減 速 機 關 鍵 零 組 件 、 機 器 手 臂 關 節
精 密 機 械 加 工 零 件 。 時 碩 目 前 已 打 入 美 國 與 中 國 廠 商 的 供 應 鏈
體 系 ； 中 國 無 錫 廠 藉 由 地 緣 優 勢 積 極 切 入 關 節 零 件 製 造 。 泰 國
春 武 里 新 廠也 規 劃 將 產 線 逐 步 延 伸 至 機 器 人 相 關 應 用 領 域 。 
3Q26 營 收 明 顯 躍 升，預估 2026 、2027 年 營 運 動 能 向 上：雖 1H26
獲 利 表 現 平 平 ， 但 受 惠 於 航 太 、 散 熱 應 用 出 貨 明 顯 增 長 帶 動 ，
3Q26 營收 18.18 億元， QoQ+23.8 6%、 YoY+43.23% ； 且 出 貨 動
能 有 望 延 伸 至 4Q26 。2026 年 方 面 ，汽 車 、 航 太 、 工 業 三 大 領 域
皆有望較 2025 年 增 長。汽 車 應 用 方 面， 雖 中 國 市 場 競 爭 仍 然 激
烈，但 因 切 入 新 能 源 車 領 域 且 客 戶 需 求 帶 動 下，營 收穩 定 增 長。
航太領域 方面， 天 陽 航 太 已 通 過 多 項 航 太 認 證 ， 成 功 打 入 全 球
前 兩 大 飛 機 製 造 商 及 Tier 1 供 應 商 ， 預 估 營 收 將維 持 高 速 成 長
態勢 。 工 業 應 用 方 面 ， 既 有 領 域 有 望 較2025 年 改 善 ， 且 新 成 長
動 能 將 來 自 於 液 冷 散 熱 相 關 產 品 及 人 形 機 器 人 零 組 件 。 預 估
2026 年 營 收 66.00 億 元 ，YoY+ 32.62 %， 毛 利 率 維 持 高 檔 ， 營 業
利益 YoY+ 97.15 %。 預 估 稅 後 純 益 3.71 億 元 ， YoY+ 148.83 %，
EPS 3.36 元。 隨 著 航 太 、 散 熱 應 用 持 續 發 酵 ， 且 泰 國 新 廠 建 置
完 成 ， 預 估 2027 年 營 收 、 獲 利 動 能 向 上 。

2 群益投顧 
  時碩 (4566  TT) E SG ：  
時碩的 ESG 政 策 由 「 永 續 發 展 暨 風 險 管 理 委 員 會 」 統 籌 ， 並 由 「 風 險 管 理
推 動 委 員 會 」 負 責 執 行 與 追 蹤，將 氣 候 變 遷 及 各 項 永 續 議 題 納 入 公 司 整 體 策
略 與 風 險 管 理 流 程 中。在 環 境 面，公 司 依 循 國 際 標 準 進 行 溫 室 氣 體 盤 查 與 第
三 方 確 信 ， 並 透 過 節 能 設 備 導 入 、 能 源 管 理 與 監 測 系 統 、 製 程 優 化 、 太 陽 能
及綠電等多元措施推動減碳與能源效率提升，同時強化水資源與廢棄物管
理 ， 藉 由 回 收 再 利 用 、 廢 水 循 環 及 資 源 化 設 備 ， 落 實 減 量 及 循 環 經 濟 ； 原 物
料 與 包 材 則 遵 循 RoHS 、REACH 等 規 範，降 低 製 程 對 環 境 的 衝 擊。社 會 面 方
面，時 碩 重 視 員 工 薪 資 福 利、培 訓 與 職 涯 發 展，推 動 性 別 平 等 與 管 理 階 層 多
元 化，並 建 立 職 業 安 全 衛 生 制 度 及 教 育 訓 練，以 降 低 職 災 風 險；在 供 應 鏈 則
透 過 定 期 稽 核 與 評 分 機 制，提 升 供 應 商 在 品 質、交 期 與 服 務 上 的 表 現，並 以
產 品 品 質 與 安 全 為 核 心 ， 確 保 生 產 與 產 品 符 合 相 關 要 求 。 同 時 ， 公 司 以「 企
業 公 民 」 自 我 定 位，投 入 社 會 公 益 與 在 地 關 懷，減 少 營 運 對 社 區 的 負 面 影 響，
並 維 持 完 善 的 資 訊 安 全 管 理 以 避 免 資 訊 外 洩。在 治 理 面，時 碩 建 構 包 含 獨 立
董 事 與 女 性 董 事 的 董 事 會 結 構，設 置 薪 酬 與 審 計 等 功 能 性 委 員 會，訂 定 涵 蓋
營 運 、 財 務、 法 遵 、 環 境 與 氣 候 變 遷 、 人 資 與 人 權 等 面 向 的 風 險 管 理 政 策 ，
並 透 過 法 說 會、網 站 與 永 續 報 告 與 利 害 關 係 人溝 通，同 時 積 極 參 與 各 項 ESG
獎 項 評 選 ， 以 強 化 公 司 治 理 與 永 續 形 象 。

6 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15 %

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 ， 亦 將 出 具 降 評 報 告 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

營收走勢圖

研究員/丁宥霖 yulin.ting@uni-psg.com 
       廖婉婷 TINA_LIAO@uni-psg.com

臻鼎 9 月營收 263.58 億元 ， 改寫單月新高，3Q26 營收 646.24 
億元，優於研究部原估的 607.09 億元，主因 AI 相關業務出貨
優於預期 ， 加上手機客戶進入新品旺季 ， 研究部據此上修 3Q26 稅
後 EPS 至 3.84 元（原估 3.47 元）。展望 4Q26，行動通訊旺
季延續， 加上 GPU 與 ASIC 新一代主板開始放量 ，營收與獲利將
續創新高 。2027 年伺服器與光模塊隨新平台出貨擴大及 1.6T 成
為主流， 營收可望各再增逾百億元；IC 載板亦受惠供給持續吃緊
及新產能陸續開出 ， 帶動 AI 相關營收占比突破三成 。 綜上所述 ，
研究部上修臻鼎 2026、2027 年稅後 EPS 至 12.11 元（+99.6% 
YoY，原估 11.81 元）及 26.14 元（+118.6% YoY，原估 22.91 
元）。基於臻鼎產品橫跨軟板、類載板、HDI、HLC 與 IC 載板，
可為客戶提供一站式解決方案 ， 並提前掌握關鍵材料與設備產能 ，
在高階 PCB 供給吃緊下擴產得以大量開出搶奪市占，研究部維
持以 2027 年獲利為評價基礎及 28 倍本益比，目標價上調至 
730 元，維持買進投資評等。

重點摘要： 
1. 9 月營收改寫新高，上修 2H26 獲利預估 
臻鼎 9 月營收 263.58 億元（+27.5% MoM、+36.1% YoY），改寫
單月新高 ， 其中伺服器 、 光模塊與 IC 載板合計營收年增逾三倍 ，
行動通訊亦呈雙位數月增 與年增 。3Q26 營收 646.24 億元
（+33.4% QoQ、+36.4% YoY），達研究部原估的 106.4%，明顯優
於預期 ， 主因 GPU 新一代主板進入量產 、1.6T 光模塊出貨放大 ，
加上手機客戶進入新品旺季。考量 AI 相關產品占比提升，研究
部預估 3Q26 毛利率為 25.5%（+2.4ppt QoQ、+3.5ppt YoY，原
估 24.7%），稅後 EPS 上修至 3.84 元（+81.9% QoQ 、+81.9% 
YoY，原估 3.47 元） 。展望 4Q26，行動通訊旺季延續，加上 GPU 
與 ASIC 新一代主板放量，研究部預估 4Q26 營收 736.13 億元
（+13.9% QoQ、+29.4% YoY），稅後 EPS 4.75 元（+23.8% QoQ、
+70.4% YoY，原估 4.86 元），營收與獲利續創新高。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

2. AI 伺服器主板成為最主要成長動能 
AI 伺服器主板隨 GPU 與 ASIC 平台升級，晶片封裝尺寸、I/O 
數與傳輸速率同步提升，主板須在有限面積內完成更多高速訊號
的扇出與繞線。 傳統 HLC 以一次壓合搭配機械鑽孔與背鑽 ，層間
連接密度已近極限；高階 HDI 則以多次壓合與雷射盲孔逐層堆
疊，線路更細、訊號路徑更短，可降低高速傳輸損耗，帶動 GPU 
平台 OAM 等主板設計由 HLC 轉向 HDI。HDI 須投入大量雷射鑽
孔機與多次壓合產能，在設備與材料交期拉長下，全球高階  AI 
PCB 產能缺口約 15–20%， 訂單已排至 2028 年，2027–2028 年
產品報價亦將持續上調。臻鼎提早取得全球逾五成高階雷射鑽孔
機產能 ， 在建 22 座新廠中半數投入 HDI、 伺服器與光模塊產品 ，
在同業擴產受限下得以持續承接訂單，帶動伺服器 HDI 比重由 
1H26 的約五成於 2H26 明顯提升；繼 ASIC 專案今年大量出貨
後，GPU 平台新一代主板亦自 4Q26 起放量。研究部預估臻鼎伺
服器營收 2026、2027 年各增加逾 100 億元 ， 為未來兩年最主要
成長動能。

3. 臻鼎可望躍居 1.6T 光模塊 PCB 龍頭 
AI 伺服器傳輸速率由 800G 升級至 1.6T，單通道速率倍增使線
路須更細 、 阻抗控制更精準 ， 傳統減成法已難以達到 ， 光模塊 PCB 
因此改採 mSAP 製程。臻鼎過去為手機類載板主要供應商，具備 
mSAP 大量生產經驗 ； 在高階設備交期拉長 、 關鍵材料供給吃緊下 ，
臻鼎已掌握市場上多數高階設備產能並持續擴充，同時提前鎖定
上游材料，得以在淮安與泰國同步擴充光模塊產能，研究部推估
臻鼎可望在 2027 年 1.6T 世代成為全球光模塊 PCB 龍頭。隨 
1.6T 出貨占比由 2026 年的約五成提升至 2027 年逾七成，研
究部預估臻鼎光模塊營收由 2025 年約 9 億元躍升至 2026 年
逾 100 億元，2027 年再增至逾 250 億元。

### Page 3

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  3

4. ABF 與 BT 同步吃緊，新產能與轉單推升載板營收 
AI 晶片封裝面積與層數持續放大，ABF 載板用量與製程難度同
步提升，惟新廠建置期長、上游材料吃緊，供需缺口持續擴大；
同業亦將部分 BT 產能轉往 ABF，使 BT 載板同步出現缺貨，兩
者報價自下半年起持續上漲。臻鼎 ABF 載板技術規格已達最大 
158×158mm、 最高 28 層 ， 深圳一廠 1Q26 轉盈並將於年底滿載 ，
二廠 1Q27 開始貢獻，第二園區 2028 年起約每年開出一座新
廠，且新產能幾乎全由國際客戶預先包下，高雄廠亦已量產先進
封裝相關載板。BT 載板雖無新建廠計畫， 但近期承接同業轉單並
調漲報價 ， 加上產品與客戶組合持續優化 ， 可望維持雙位數成長 。
隨 ABF 產能接續開出，研究部預估 ABF 占載板營收比重將由 
2025 年的 35% 提升至 2027 年約七成，IC 載板營收 2026、
2027 年分別成長 99.2% 及 89.0%。

5. 高階新產能陸續開出，產品組合優化推升毛利率 
臻鼎 2026 年資本支出逾 800 億元，2027 年維持相當水準，廠
房數將由目前的 28 座擴增至 2030 年底的 59 座。新產能自 
2027 年起陸續開出：淮安 6 座新廠於 2026 年底完工後進入裝
機，HD 園區預計 3M27 投產、2Q27 大批量產；泰國新廠於 4Q26 
起試產，1H27 進入小量生產 ； 深圳第三園區與載板第二園區則預
計自 2028 年起接棒 （表二） 。新廠初期稼動率與良率仍在爬坡，
加上 2027 年折舊費用預計較 2026 年再增加 20% 至 30%，短
期將稀釋毛利率；惟新增產能以 AI 伺服器、光模塊與 IC 載板
為主，隨 AI 相關營收占比由 2025 年的 11.7% 提升至 2027 
年突破三成，研究部預估臻鼎毛利率將由 2026 年的 24.5% 提
升至 2027 年的 29.3%。

6. 上修 2026、2027 年獲利預估 
2026 年 AI 相關業務為主要成長動能 ，研究部預估伺服器 、光模
塊與其他營收成長 269.0%，IC 載板成長 99.2%，行動通訊受惠
摺疊機新品成長 6.9%，電腦消費則與 2025 年持平，AI 相關營
收占比將由 2025 年的 11.7% 提升至 25.9%。研究部預估 2026 
年營收 2,273.96 億元（+24.6% YoY，原估 2,267.70 億元），
毛利率由 24.1% 上修至 24.5%（+4.7ppt YoY ），稅後 EPS 由 
11.81 元上修至 12.11 元（+99.6% YoY）。 
展望 2027 年，伺服器與光模塊隨新平台出貨擴大及 1.6T 成為
主流，營收合計成長逾一倍，IC 載板受惠 AI 客戶訂單放量、
ABF 比重提升及報價調漲，成長 89.0%，行動通訊與電腦消費則
分別維持中、低個位數成長。在高毛利產品占比提升、費用率隨
營收規模擴大回落，抵銷新廠折舊增加下，研究部預估 2027 年
營收 3,158.99 億元（+38.9% YoY，原估 3,083.20 億元），毛
利率提升至 29.3%（+4.8ppt YoY，原估 28.1%） ，營益率提升至 
17.9%（原估 14.8%） ， 稅後 EPS 上修至 26.14 元（+118.6% YoY，
原估 22.91 元）。

## Document 183: 6467泰合 永豐

Report source: line報告備份

### Page 1

個股聚焦  
2026/10/20

陳奕均 fion.chen@sinopac.com

泰合 (6467 TT) 
開花結果在即

永豐觀點 
抗血栓口溶膜貼片 BEQIA 歐美取證在即。 
 投資評價與建議 
投資建議買進：泰合 10/20 上市掛牌，掛牌價105.6 元。由於(1) 泰合開發之口溶
膜劑型藥物有效解決高齡與吞嚥困難之臨床痛點下，市場競爭力足；(2) 抗血栓口
溶膜貼片 BEQIA 歐美取證在即，2027 年在授權與上市權利金開始認列下 ，營運轉
虧為盈且呈大跳躍狀；(3) 由於多數學名藥廠受限專利訴訟，最快需至 2028 年 4
月後才可上市銷售，促使泰合享有約一年的「黃金市場空窗期」 ，能與原廠直接瓜
分市場。且因 BEQIA 屬 505(b)(2)新劑型新藥，即便2Q28 後學名藥陸續進場，其
獨特的劑型優勢仍能築起價格防護牆，所面臨的價折壓力相對輕微，建議買進，目
標價 170 元( 18 X 2027 EPS )。

營運現況與分析 
公司簡介：泰合生技成立於 2010 年，專注開發 505(b)(2)新劑型新藥與特殊學名
藥，核心技術為自主研發的 TDS 透皮／透黏膜傳輸平台，將現有口服藥品成分改
造為透皮與透黏膜藥物傳導劑型 ，提供方便使用且更安全的新劑型藥物。 產品開發
上，505(b)(2)部分，治療抗血栓口溶膜貼片(每日兩片)BEQIA 靜待歐美取證中外，
其餘則仍在臨床階段中，困難學名藥部分，化療止吐口溶膜2018 年日本取證銷售
中外，其餘四項藥品則在進行洽談授權合作。 
圖一：泰合產品線開發時程

資料來源：泰合；永豐投顧研究部整理，Oct. 2026

### Page 2

2

口溶膜劑型藥物能有效解決高齡與吞嚥困難之臨床痛點 ：傳統口服錠劑藥物必須經
過食道、進入胃腸道消化，再經由肝臟代謝後才進入血液循環，泰合開發之口溶膜
劑型藥物， 可讓藥膜在接觸舌頭或口腔黏膜數秒內快速溶解 ， 病患完全不需飲水即
可完成給藥， 且部份藥物分子能直接透過口腔黏膜上皮細胞微血管吸收， 直接進入
全身血液循環， 不僅能提高藥物生體可用率， 還能減少胃酸對藥物的破壞與對胃腸
道的刺激下，有效解決高齡與吞嚥困難之臨床痛點，提高患者服藥順從性。 
抗血栓口溶膜貼片 BEQIA 歐美取證在即，提前卡位下，營運動能可期：泰合自主
開發的抗血栓口溶膜新劑型新藥 BEQIA， 預計4Q26 取得歐盟藥證 、2Q27 獲美國
FDA 核准上市。目前該藥原廠在美國市場年銷售額>200 億美元，相較原廠口服錠
劑，泰合 BEQIA 具備三大競爭優勢：不需飲水（服藥便利）、解決吞嚥困難（減
輕照顧端壓力），以及攜帶與給藥便利（優化長照與醫療機構給藥流程）。 
市場競爭方面，由於多數學名藥廠受限專利訴訟，最快需至2028 年 4 月後才可上
市銷售，促使泰合享有約一年的「黃金市場空窗期」 ，能與原廠直接瓜分市場。此
外，由於BEQIA 屬 505(b)(2)新劑型新藥，即便2Q28 後學名藥陸續進場，其獨特
的劑型優勢仍能築起價格防護牆，所面臨的折價壓力應相對輕微。 
泰合海外市場營銷預計將採授權分潤模式，研究部預估 1H27 歐美地區簽約金應
有機會各達 500~1000 萬美金外，隨美國地區 2Q27 上市、通路鋪設及醫保陸續
准入，Peak sales 市佔率 2-3%(預期將落於 2029-30 年)、銷售分潤 12-15%估算
下，稅後 EPS 貢獻將落於 10-20 元。 
財務預估：預估泰合 2026 年營收 0.1 億元，稅後虧損 1.89 億元，稅後 EPS 以掛
牌後股本 5.99 億元估算，稅後 EPS-3.15 元，2027 年預估歐美地區授權簽約金各
500 萬美金及 1000 萬美金 ， 及上市銷售後權利金認列下 ，預估營收將跳升至7.75
億元，稅後淨利 5.68 億元，稅後 EPS 9.49 元。

重點摘要： 
 2Q26 營收 37.4 億，YoY +42.8% ，毛 利 率34.05%，EPS 12.39
元。 1H26 毛利率  33.13% EPS 25.77 元。  
 隨著 VR200 與 ASIC 伺服器專案陸續放量 ， 伺服器產品比重提
升，再加上新產能第四季陸續開出，公司營收、毛利將維持成
長動能。  
 2027 年受惠於 Vera Rubin 平台與 ASIC 進入放量階段，下一
世代 Vera Rubin Ultra 機櫃，預期將導入更高規格 ZQD ，將
有望帶動單櫃快接頭產值提升。

一. 2Q26 毛利率達  34.05% ，EPS 12.39 元 
富世達 2Q26 營收 37.4 億， QoQ -10.6% ，YoY +42.8% ，營收較
前一季下滑主要由於手機、伺服器產品處於新舊產品轉換期影
響。而獲利方面， 2Q26 營業毛利 13.5 億， QoQ -5.8% ，YoY 
+101.6% ，毛利率 34.05% ，QoQ +1.74ppts ，YoY +9.98ppts ，
2Q26 營業利益 10.4 億，QoQ -6.9% ，YoY +109.9% ，公司表示，
受惠於製程持續優化、伺服器產品佔比提升至五成以上，帶動毛
利率提升 。2Q26 業外淨利 0.3 億 ， 稅後淨利8.49 億，QoQ -7.4% ，
YoY +157.3% ，單季稅後 EPS 12.39 元。

二. 預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 63.08  
公司公告 9 月營收 16.13 億， MoM +5.6% ，YoY +32.2% ，3Q26
營收 45.02 億，QoQ +36.1% ，YoY +79.3% ，今年累計營收 124.3
億， YoY +47.3% 。展望 2H26 ，公司預期隨著 VR200 與 ASIC 伺
服器專案陸續放量，水冷快接頭與滑軌的出貨比重將顯著提升，
再加上新產能第四季將陸續開出，公司營收將維持成長動能。而
摺疊手機部分，受惠於客戶發布新款產品，亦將推升摺疊手機業
務回溫。此外，由於高毛利伺服器產品比重提升，公司預期毛利
率將持續攀升，且營業費用率亦將隨營收規模擴大而逐漸下降。
預估 2026 全年營收 181.8 億元， YoY +46.5% ，毛利率 35.2% ，
營利率 29.4% ，稅後淨利 43.2 億，全年稅後 EPS 63.08 元。

三. 預估 2027 年稅後 EPS 112.24 元 
展望 2027 年，受惠於 Vera Rubin 平台與 ASIC 進入主要放量階
段， QD 應用亦從 Switch Tray 延伸至 Compute Tray 及 Rack 
Manifold ， 將帶動機櫃快接頭用量提升 。 此外 ， 由於晶片功耗提升 、
投資評等：買進

冷卻液流量需求增加， NVIDIA 下一世代的 Vera Rubin Ultra 機
櫃，預期將導入更高規格 ZQD ，單價預估將比現行的 MQD/UQD

淨利 76.9 億，全年稅後 EPS 112.24 元。

四. 投資建議為「買進」，目標價 2920 元 
考量到 AI 伺服器水冷滲透率持續提升， VR 及 ASIC 專案的強勁
需求，公司快接頭 (QD) 產品成長動能明確，公司受惠於各項專案
的陸續發酵，將顯著推升公司伺服器產品的營收貢獻，亦有望帶
動公司營收與獲利維持成長動能。目前股價交易於 2027 年 EPS
約 21~22 倍本益比，評價仍具上修空間，給於買進投資建議，目
標價 2920 元(26x 2027 EPS) 。

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介  
富世達 (FOSITEK) 成立於 2001 年，為奇鋐科技 (3017) 旗下子公
司，主要從事電子類、電腦、資訊通信產業等 3C 電子產品轉軸組
件之研發、製造及銷售。公司產品主要應用於電腦、電子、光學、
家電、資訊、通信、醫療設備等產品之連結軸，如商用 NB 轉軸、
工業用 NB 轉軸 、 軍事用NB 轉軸 、LCD Monitor 轉軸 、PDA 轉軸、
LCD-TV 轉軸、手機轉軸、螢幕懸臂支架、醫療懸臂支架等。公司
初期與京東方、華為三方合作生產研發摺疊手機，為華為摺疊手
機的主要開案合作廠商。近年積極投入水冷快接頭模組量產，並
也陸續送給鴻海、廣達等組裝廠進行測試，同時也通過數家水冷
板供應商認證，已成功打入輝達 GB200 與 GB300 供應鏈出貨水
冷快接頭 (QD/UQD) 與滑軌， 2025 年折疊手機佔公司整體營收的
56.4%， 筆記型電腦佔6.4%， 伺服器產品佔36.3%， 其他佔0.9%。
公司總部位於台北新莊新北產業園區，並於大陸深圳及越南設有
生產據點。公司 AI 伺服器與水冷零件主要客戶為 NVIDIA 及 CSP
大廠；手機主要客戶包含華為、 Motorola ，Google 等；筆電客戶
包含 Dell 、HP、聯想、華為。而公司主要競爭對手則包含新日興
(3376) 、兆利科技 (3548) 等。

二.產業趨勢概況  
1. 四大 CSP 上修今年資本支出達 7,450 億美元 
在生成式 AI 商機快速擴張帶動下，全球雲端基礎設施投資進入新
一輪高峰。亞馬遜、 Alphabet 和微軟認為其在先前的鉅額投資已
帶動營收明顯成長 ， 同時預估未來AI 潛在商機仍持續擴大 。 其中 ，
亞馬遜、 Alphabet 和 Meta 已陸續上調 2026 全年投資金額，且四
大雲端服務供應商 (CSP) 總資本支出將上升至 7,450 億美元新高
規模。亞馬遜、微軟、 Alphabet 與 Meta 2026 年第 1、2 季分別
為 1,306 億、1,701 億美元，同時亞馬遜、 Alphabet 和 Meta 再上
修 2026 全年資本支出金額， 2026 年資本支出預估將達 7,450 億
美元。而根據 TrendForce 最新產業研究，全球主要雲端服務供應
商(CSP) 為加速建置 AI 基礎設施，資本支出預估於 2026 年將年
增 98%，2027 年再成長 50% 至 1.3 兆美元左右，年增率收斂的主
要原因，除了反映高基期因素之外，部分可歸咎為鉅額資本支出
對四大 CSP 財務衝擊日益擴大。然而，在 AI Inference 、Agentic 
AI、自研 ASIC 和新一代 AI 模型持續推升算力需求的情況下，預
期整體 CSP 投資金額將續創新高紀錄，產業成長有望從原先以
GPU 擴張為主，轉為包含 AI server 、液冷散熱、先進封裝、高速
互聯、電源和記憶體等基礎設施全面升級的態勢。

2. 2026 年全球 AI 伺服器出貨量預計年增 31% 
根據 TrendForce 最新 AI server 產業研究，近期超大型 CSP 、
Tier-2 資料中心業者對 NVIDIA GB/VR 等整櫃式 AI server 方案的
採購意願明顯提高，且 Google 、AWS 新一代 ASIC 平台於 2026
下半年陸續放量，加上中系業者擴大採用本地 AI 方案以滿足雲端
大型語言模型 (LLM) AI 需求，促使 TrendForce 上調 2026 年 AI 
server 出貨年成長幅度，從原先的 28% 更新為近 31%。北美五大
業者 2026 年的資本支出多翻倍提升，反映出大型 CSP 廠持續擴
建 AI 資料中心、 GPU 叢集、液冷散熱等新一代基礎設施，以應對
生成式 AI 及大型模型快速成長帶來的算力需求。其中， Google 
2026 至 2027 年都將側重自家 TPU 晶片發展， 2027 年出貨量有
望繼續高速成長。 AWS 2026 年以 NVIDIA GB300 作為 GPU AI 
server 主力，自研 ASIC 也穩定出貨，預期至 2027 年量能將再擴
大。 Meta 2026 年主要採用 NVIDIA GB/VR 、AMD Helios 整櫃式
方案，自研 ASIC 布局預計至 2027 年將明顯加速，為該公司長期
降低 AI 基礎設施成本、提升推論效率的重要策略。

3.液冷散熱成高階 AI 基礎設施標配，估 2026 年滲透率達 53% 
根據 TrendForce 最新研究顯示，隨著 AI 基礎設施建置浪潮及高
階晶片運算功率不斷攀升，單晶片熱設計功耗已超越 1kW ，使液
冷散熱技術正式成為高階 AI 基礎設施的標準配置。在 NVIDIA 、
AMD 與 Google 等巨頭的高階晶片與整櫃式方案強力推動下，預
估 2026 年液冷散熱於 AI 晶片的滲透率將從 2025 年的 33% 大幅
躍升至 53%，並在 2027 年進一步逼近 60%。其中， Google 因積
極導入自研 TPU 並率先全面推廣整櫃式液冷設計，成為主要推手
之一。此外，隨著 NVIDIA Vera Rubin 平台全面導入全液冷架構，
水冷板、分歧管及冷卻分配系統等關鍵零組件需求隨之大增，帶

4. 蘋果入局帶動摺疊機高成長，估今年全球摺疊機出貨年增 24% 
根據 Counterpoint Research 報告 ， 全球摺疊智慧型手機累計出貨
量預計將於 2026 年底突破 1 億支大關，達成自 2019 年首度問世
以來的重大里程碑。隨著消費者對大尺寸書本式摺疊機的需求攀
升，加上蘋果 (Apple) 推出首款摺疊 iPhone 的帶動，預估 2026 年
全球摺疊機出貨量將年增 24% ，2027 年出貨預估年增 37% 。而
根據 Counterpoint Research 預估 ， 三星(Samsung) 憑藉 Galaxy Z 
Fold8 等大尺寸書本式設計的強勁動能 ， 預計在2026 年將以  38%
的市占率穩居全球龍頭寶座；而蘋果 (Apple) 推出的首款摺疊
iPhone ，雖預期在初期會面臨產能限制，但仍將為高階市場帶來
立即的衝擊，預估 2026 年就能迅速搶下 25% 的市占率空降全球
第二；而華為 (Huawei) 則預估以 22% 的市占率位居第三，主要由
於其在中國市場依然保持強勢的主導地位，並受到  Pura X Max 
等產品的推動。

圖五、 2026 年全球摺疊手機市占率預估

資料來源： Counterpoint 、宏遠投顧整理

叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估

一. 預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 63.08  
公司公告 9 月營收 16.13 億， MoM +5.6% ，YoY +32.2% ，3Q26
營收 45.02 億，QoQ +36.1% ，YoY +79.3% ，今年累計營收 124.3
億， YoY +47.3% 。展望 2H26 ，公司預期隨著 VR200 與 ASIC 伺
服器專案陸續放量，水冷快接頭與滑軌的出貨比重將顯著提升，
再加上新產能第四季將陸續開出，公司營收將維持成長動能。而
摺疊手機部分，受惠於客戶發布新款產品，亦將推升摺疊手機業
務回溫。此外，由於高毛利伺服器產品比重提升，公司預期毛利
率將持續攀升，且營業費用率亦將隨營收規模擴大而逐漸下降。
預估 2026 全年營收 181.8 億元， YoY +46.5% ，毛利率 35.2% ，
營利率 29.4% ，稅後淨利 43.2 億，全年稅後 EPS 63.08 元。

二. 預估 2027 年稅後 EPS 112.24 元 
展望 2027 年，受惠於 Vera Rubin 平台與 ASIC 進入主要放量階
段， QD 應用亦從 Switch Tray 延伸至 Compute Tray 及 Rack 
Manifold ， 將帶動機櫃快接頭用量提升 。 此外 ， 由於晶片功耗提升 、
冷卻液流量需求增加， NVIDIA 下一世代的 Vera Rubin Ultra 機
櫃，預期將導入更高規格 ZQD ，單價預估將比現行的 MQD/UQD
顯著提升，將有望帶動單櫃快接頭產值提升。預估 2027 全年營收
295.5 億元， YoY +46.5% ，毛利率 37.6% ，營利率 32.3% ，稅後
淨利 76.9 億，全年稅後 EPS 112.24 元。

## Document 219: [207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 불리한 환경에서도 성장성 유지삼성바이오로직스(207940) 불리한 환경에서도 성장성 유지불리한 환경에_79fc85e4

3Q26Re: 신규 시설 매출 인식 개시 
연결 기준 매출액 1조 4,903억원(+18.5%YoY, 이하 YoY 생략), 영업이익 6,401억원
(+1.0%, OPM 42.9%)로 컨센서스를 상회하는 실적 기록. 1~4공장이 완전 가동을 유지하는 
한편, 5공장은 PPQ 배치 생산 본격화되면서 매출 기여 확대. 상업 생산 전환이 예상되는 
2H27부터 이익 기여 커질 것으로 기대. 
연초 인수한 Rockville 공장은 3Q에도 안정적인 상업 생산을 이어갔으며 2Q 생산 물량의 
매출이 인식되며 매출 성장에 기여. 동사는 Rockville 공장 매출 인식 등을 반영하여 기존 
+15~20% 성장에서 +20%로 연간 가이던스 상향.   
고객사 수주 회복 기대 
3Q26 기준 누적 수주액은 219억 달러로 전분기 대비 소폭 증가했으나 과거 연간 수주액 
증가 대비 부족한 수준. 저조한 수주 실적은 지난해 초 의약품에 대한 관세 불확실성 증가로 
고객사들의 수주 문의가 저조했던 영향. 26년 초부터 관세 불확실성이 낮아지며 수주 문의
는 회복 추세. 약 1년 정도 논의 기간이 소요되는 것을 고려할 때 26년말~27년 초 수주 회
복 확인 가능할 것으로 기대. 
안정적인 공급망 유지가 중요한 CDMO 산업 특성상 노조 파업 가능성도 고객사 수주를 늦
추는 요인으로 판단. 다만, 10/16 인천 고등법원이 쟁의행위 가처분 항고심에 대해 인용 결
정을 하며 기존 3개 공정에서 9개 공정으로 확대되며 파업 우려는 다소 완화. 
투자 의견 Buy, 목표 주가 200만원 유지 
매수 의견과 목표 주가 200만원을 유지. 원달러 약세에도 불구하고 5공장 Ramp-up과 
Rockville 공장 매출 기여로 견조한 실적 성장 지속될 것으로 예상. 파업 이슈 해소와 대형 
수주 계약이 주가 상승을 이끌 것으로 판단.

## Document 310: Federal Reserve preview_ A pause before a December hike

Source PDF: all report pdf/Federal Reserve preview_ A pause before a December hike.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-10-23
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Despite hot inﬂation prints in September and the Fed having
signalled that further action to tame inﬂation will likely be
needed, markets and economists are united in expecting the
central bank to leave interest rates on hold at the 28 October
FOMC meeting. Ongoing concerns about elevated price
pressures mean we do expect a December interest rate rise

James Knightley, Padhraic Garvey, CFA and Chris Turner
The Fed looks set to hold rates steady at the October FOMC meeting before
hiking again in December
Fed ofﬁcials play for time
The immediate reaction to the surprise September 0.3% month-on-
month core CPI print was to send the chances of an October rate
hike above 50%. But this didn’t last long. Earlier comments from
Federal Reserve Vice Chairs Philip Jeﬀerson and Michelle Bowman
and Governor Chris Waller, signalling no rush to decide on the next
policy move, quickly calmed markets. The fact that Fed Chair Kevin
Warsh subsequently chose not to talk up the chances of action
when he spoke at the IMF meetings in Bangkok, unlike at the

### Page 2

Jackson Hole Conference before the September hike, further
dampened the perceived chances of action.
While it is true to say that recent jobs numbers have been
disappointing and interest rate-sensitive sectors, such as the
housing market, are experiencing increasing pain, the economy is
growing robustly, and inﬂation remains above target. Fed oﬃcials
themselves have made it clear that they think more tightening of
monetary policy is going to be required to bring inﬂation to heel.

Given that the latest Federal Reserve Summary of Economic
Projections showed four FOMC members projecting 50bp of rate
hikes between now and the end of the year, the decision is unlikely
to be unanimous. Beth Hammack, Neel Kashkari and Lorie Logan
all voted to hike in July when the rest of the committee wanted to
hold steady. All three could again dissent in favour of hiking.
The press release is likely to be brief, and we also expect another
short and snappy press conference from Chair Warsh. He's likely to

keep things quite matter-of-fact given his lack of enthusiasm for
forward guidance. We expect his message to be that the economy
remains in decent shape, unemployment is low, and inﬂation is
above target. The policy changes being made will be determined
by the trends in incoming data, with oﬃcials currently thinking that
they probably have more work to do.
US core consumer price inﬂation YoY%
versus the Fed funds policy rate

Looking towards December
We will have two jobs reports and one inﬂation print ahead of the
December decision. The November CPI will be released the day after
the Fed meeting, and oﬃcials will almost certainly get to see it. As
such, they will have more information than the market does,

meaning a surprise can’t be completely ruled out.
We are forecasting a 25bp rate hike at the December meeting
(leaving the Fed funds target rate range at 4-4.25%) followed by
a prolonged pause through to December 2027, when we have that
December 2026 hike being reversed. This is in line with the current
consensus forecast, while the Fed’s own projections (its central
forecast) see one hike this year with rates left unchanged
throughout 2027.
While the sharp move higher in Treasury yields and the ﬁrmer

dollar are tightening ﬁnancial conditions to a degree, our forecast
path for the Fed assumes that in the couple of months following
the midterm elections we see an agreement that improves the ﬂow
of energy from the Persian Gulf, but signiﬁcantly, also allows for a
sharp reduction in shipping and insurance costs. It's the latter point
that is crucial to delivering lower pump prices at a time when
reﬁneries are working at capacity. If correct, this should

meaningfully ease inﬂation pressures, and with US wage growth
(the biggest cost input for corporate America) looking very benign
at just 3% and the largest component of the inﬂation basket –
housing at 35% by weight – set to experience more disinﬂation, we

### Page 4

believe overall US inﬂation can fall back to 2% in the second half of
2027.
The risk to our view is clearly tilted towards a longer-lasting conﬂict
rather than one that is resolved imminently. This means we would
not rule out additional hike(s) in 1Q 2027, but with households
experiencing a greater squeeze on spending power (weak wage
growth amid rising energy costs) and bond yields likely pushing
even higher – leading to new cycle highs for mortgage rates and

corporate borrowing costs – the risks of a weaker economy will
increase. As such, in this more stressed economic scenario,
eventual cuts to policy rates will likely be swifter and more
substantial in 2H 2027 than in our base case.
The Fed to leave well enough alone and let
Treasuries do their own thing
A solid performance for US Treasuries in the past week has acted to
reduce the prior tightening impulse that had gripped the market.
The 10yr yield below 5% obviously contrasts dramatically with the

prior break above 5.25%. We’re not convinced the more recent
downward lurch is persisting. But its genesis lends itself to a softer
approach from the FOMC at this meeting.
The risk-oﬀ tone creates a heightened risk proﬁle for the US
economy, raising the probability that the Fed holds at this meeting.
The battering that the French bond market has been taking is
another important impulse, as it heightens the broader sense of
bond angst. And at the margin, it can add to systemic pressure.

This is not the type of environment where the Fed would be inclined
to add to concerns by tightening policy. It’s the perfect meeting to
keep rates on hold.
Whether we remain below 5% for the 10yr yield is a whole other
question. We're minded to expect it to snap back above, as we still
have inﬂation, ﬁscal deﬁcit, and AI-boom impulses at play. The Fed
will no doubt remind us of these ongoing pressures. Plus, a break
back above 5% will likely need a degree of calm in the risk space

ﬁrst. We think it should ultimately move back above 5%; the timing,

however, is a tougher call. That being said, this is not the meeting

We’re likely still some way from the Fed spelling out a plan to shrink

Chair Warsh. We expect recommendations from the various
committees to surface closer to the turn of the year. Meanwhile,
liquidity circumstances are stable, with repo behaving far better
than it did a year ago.

Warsh FOMC meetings have set the dollar
tone since June
The dollar should be able to hold on to most of its September
gains, as Fed communication is likely to remain supportive of
higher US yields. If the dollar is to correct lower, a renewed tech-led
risk-oﬀ move rather than a dovish shift in Fed messaging is the
more likely catalyst.
After a sharp 4% run higher through September and into early
October, the dollar has entered a more consolidative phase. Rate

diﬀerentials are the most important driver at the moment, and
these have moved slightly against the dollar this month as the US
tech sell-oﬀ has pared back Fed tightening expectations.
Broadening out the lens, it looks like Warsh-led FOMC meetings
have set the four-to-six-week trend for the dollar this year. June’s
meeting was hawkish, July’s was seen as dovish, and the Fed
delivered a hawkish hike in September.
We suspect that Warsh has learned from the criticism he received

over July's FOMC meeting, where he implied that the bond market
was doing the tightening (read as the Fed would not have to). That
probably means he will not be drawn into concluding that wider
credit spreads and softer equity prices mean tighter ﬁnancial
conditions now require fewer Fed hikes.
Assuming that Warsh sticks to the script that monetary policy
credibility remains the Fed’s top priority, short-dated US yields can
remain well-supported, and the dollar can largely hold on to the

gains it has made since early September. And the euro looks like it

## Document 313: First Impressions_ NZ Consumers Price Index Q3 2026 and change of OCR call (2026-10-28)

Source PDF: all report pdf/First Impressions_ NZ Consumers Price Index Q3 2026 and change of OCR call (2026-10-28).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-10-28
Pages: 12
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#inflation #newzealand
First Impressions: NZ Consumers Price Index Q3 2026 and change
of OCR call
By Satish Ranchhod
Senior Economist, Westpac NZ
09:19 October 22 2026 Share
Consumer prices rose 0.7% in the September quarter and 3.7% y/y. Core inflation
pressures are elevated and market pricing is primed for a 25bp OCR hike next week, which
we now expect.
Consumers Price Index, September quarter 2026 – key stats

### Page 2

Headline inflation
Quarterly change: +0.7% (prev: +1.5%)
Westpac forecast: +0.7%
RBNZ: +0.8%
Market median: +0.8%, range +0.5% to +1.2%

Annual change: +3.7% (prev: +4.1%)
Westpac forecast: +3.7%, RBNZ: +3.9%, Market: +3.8%

Non-tradables
Quarterly change: +1.0% (prev: +0.6%)
Westpac forecast: +1.3%
Annual change: +3.3% (prev: +3.4%)

Tradables
Quarterly change: +0.2% (prev: +2.7%)
Westpac forecast: -0.2%
Annual change: +4.3% (prev: +4.9%)

Consumer prices rose 0.7% in the September quarter. That saw the annual inflation rate easing to
3.7%, down from 4.1% in the year to June. 
Today’s result was in line with our forecast, but lower than the RBNZ’s forecast for 3.9% annual
inflation. 
However, under the surface, the details were more concerning. Measure of core inflation have taken a
step higher, with several closely watched measures now at or slightly above the top of the RBNZ’s
target band. 
We suspect that today’s result will leave the market continuing to price a strong chance of another
OCR hike at next Wednesday’s policy review. In that light, we think the RBNZ will regard this as the
path of least resistance, and we now expect that they will deliver (previously we expected that they
would pause and assess, before hiking at the December MPS).

What contributed to inflation in the September quarter?
Underpinning September’s 0.7% rise in consumer prices were some big price movements in both
directions. 
The largest move in prices this quarter was actually a sharp fall in fuel costs. Fuel prices aren’t low by
any stretch of the imagination. However, average prices for both petrol and diesel over the September
quarter were lower than in the June quarter when prices rocketed higher in the wake of the Middle East

### Page 3

conflict. In terms of specifics, the average price for 91 unleaded was 6.5% lower in September than in
the June quarter, while diesel prices were close to 16% lower. Those moves took 0.4ppts off inflation
this quarter. 
While prices at the pump weren’t as high as they were in the June quarter, fuel costs are still painfully
high. And that’s been bleeding through into higher prices for other goods and services. Of note, the
September quarter saw large increases in passenger transport costs, with international airfares up 14%
and domestic airfares up nearly 8%. 
The September quarter also saw the annual increases in local council rates (up 6.2%) and charges for
services like water supply (up 7.5%). That continues the run of large increases seen in recent years.
Lastly, the past few months saw a 1.0% rise in food prices, largely as a result of the seasonal rise in
prices for fresh vegetables. 
On the downside, we’ve seen continued softness in housing rents, which are the largest component of
the CPI, accounting for 11.2% of the basket. With low population growth and ample supply, rents were
flat in the September quarter and have effectively been flat since the start of this year. In annual terms,
rental inflation is the lowest it’s been in more than two decades, with regions like Wellington seeing
outright falls.

Annual and core inflation 
The annual inflation rate dropped back to 3.7% in the September quarter, down from 4.1% in the year
to June and the highest level in two years.
Looking under the surface, prices in the domestically oriented non-tradables group rose 1.0% over the
quarter, less than expected. That saw annual non-tradables inflation nudging down to 3.3% (from 3.4%
in the year to June). 
Non-tradables inflation has continued to run at above-average rates over the past year. That’s despite
the softness in economic growth and the labour market. In large part, that’s due to the continued
sizeable increases in administered prices, such as electricity charges which rose 9.3% over the past
year. 
However, prices in other areas have also been firm. Non-tradables excluding housing and utilities costs
(which also omits local council rates) was 3.4% for the year. In seasonally adjusted terms, quarterly
non-tradables inflation has been tracking around 0.8% for most of the past year. It was noted that in
sectors like construction, higher materials costs are adding to prices. Feedback from business we've
heard indicates that those higher materials costs are likely related to high oil prices. 
On the imported front, lower fuel costs saw tradable prices rising by just 0.2% over the September
quarter (though above our forecast for a small fall). That saw annual tradables inflation dropping back
to 4.3%, down sharply from the 4.9% rise in the year to June. 
While fuel costs have been a key driver for tradables inflation, under the surface, other imported
inflation pressures are looking firm. Excluding food and fuel costs, tradables prices were up 2.7% over
the past year. That’s the highest level in three years. 
With big swings in fuel costs, and concern that this is spilling over into operating costs more generally,
a key focus for the RBNZ is what’s been happening to measures of core inflation (which smooth
through quarter-to-quarter swings in individual prices and instead track the underlying trend in

### Page 4

Related articles
inflation). 
On this front we did some see worrying signs. Several closely watched measures of core inflation have
picked up, and now sit at the top of (or slightly above) the RBNZ target band. In terms of specifics, we
saw: 
Reinforcing this picture of firm underlying inflation pressures, a growing number of goods have been
increasing in price. That could be an early indication that businesses, even those with a limited
exposure to fuel costs, are starting to factor in expectations of continued high inflation when setting
prices.

Outlook 
While today’s result was lower than the RBNZ assumed in their September MPS projections, the
firmness in core inflation will give the RBNZ pause for thought ahead of their policy review on 28
October. Furthermore, with a sharp rise in petrol prices in recent weeks, September’s easing in annual
inflation is likely to be just a temporary reprieve. We expect headline inflation to reaccelerate back over
4% before the end of this year, and it’s set to remain above the RBNZ’s current forecast for most of
2027. 
Market pricing and views have coalesced around an October hike which we now suspect the MPC will
see as the path of least resistance. Core inflation measures are now close to or above 3%, which will
add fuel to arguments from the MPC hawks. We will have more detail in our MPR Preview later today,
including on the implications for the shape of the tightening cycle moving forward.

Jet fuel is often the largest or second-largest cost item for airlines.
In 2025, it accounted for 27% of total operational costs on average.
This share could move towards 35% in 2026 as a result of
persistently high jet fuel prices and elevated crack spreads, caused
by the loss of Middle Eastern supplies and tight global availability.
Further geopolitical developments remain highly uncertain. Even if
the Middle East conﬂict is resolved and oil prices retreat, tight

reﬁnery capacity and depleted inventories are likely to keep middle-
distillate prices, including jet fuel, elevated well into 2027.

Fuel hedging provides less cushioning in
2027; fares must respond
Airlines employ very diﬀerent strategies to protect themselves
against fuel price shocks, using derivatives based on crude oil or jet
fuel. European airlines generally hedged 40-90% of their jet fuel
consumption, cushioning the initial impact of the price shock.
However, the Middle East conﬂict has lasted too long to avoid a

signiﬁcant impact. Existing hedges gradually roll oﬀ, increasing
exposure to prevailing market prices. Ryanair, which remains (80%)
hedged until the second quarter of 2027, has already warned of
signiﬁcantly higher fares.
Outside, the picture is mixed, but hedge ratios are generally lower,
and airlines in the US and China, for instance, don’t hedge at all.
This means that two airlines operating similar routes can face very
diﬀerent fuel bills.
Airline tickets in the US responded much

faster to the cost shock than in Europe

Index ticket prices vs consumer price index (CPI, seasonally adjusted) in

Fares already sharply higher in the US, less
so in Europe
The fuel price shock will increasingly feed through into higher fares.
US airlines’ practice of retaining relatively high exposure to fuel price
volatility resulted in an almost immediate pass-through, with ticket
prices rising solidly in the months after jet fuel prices started to rise.

It is also easier to raise prices when domestic competitors face a
similar cost shock.
The situation is diﬀerent in Europe, where hedging provided more
initial protection. This also means fares may still need to rise
signiﬁcantly if fuel prices fail to fall. Coverage gradually expires as
2027 progresses, leaving airlines increasingly exposed to higher
spot prices.
Airline proﬁts hit, but industry remains
proﬁtable

### Page 9

Source: IATA, *ING Research based on IATA
More factors are driving up costs,
particularly in Europe
Jet fuel is the clear outlier this year, but wage and maintenance
costs are also rising faster than the average price level. Borrowing
costs and aircraft leasing rates remain another factor, although the
impact depends on individual airlines’ strategies and ﬁnancial
structures.
European airlines also face rising costs from the European Union
Emissions Trading System (ETS) for ﬂights within the European

Economic Area. In addition, fuel suppliers are required to blend
sustainable aviation fuel (SAF) under ReFuelEU Aviation, starting at
2% and rising to 6% by 2030*. SAF still trades at a signiﬁcant
premium. This puts a price on emissions, and in combination, this
adds some $300 per tonne of jet fuel in 2026 (BNEF, ING Research).
Meanwhile, France and the Netherlands have either raised or plan
to raise ticket taxes, particularly on long-haul ﬂying, while Germany

has scaled its levy back and Sweden has abolished it.
High fuel prices put more pressure on low-
cost carriers
*A revision proposal is in progress for aviation, drawing a circle of 5,000 km around
Frankfurt for application of ETS, which would hit Middle East airlines most as they will
then be in scope. **In 2025, the blend rate stood at 2.8%

### Page 10

Low-cost carriers are particularly exposed because fuel accounts
for a larger share of their cost base, and proﬁt bases are more
vulnerable. High hedge ratios initially cushion volatility, but these
hedges gradually roll oﬀ. The more challenging environment is
already visible in the performance of several European low-cost
carriers and will remain an important theme in 2027 if fuel prices
stay elevated.
Premium carriers have more room to manoeuvre. Demand for

premium seats (business, premium economy) is less price-
sensitive, providing airlines with greater scope to pass on higher
costs. Both United Airlines and Delta Airlines reported little impact
after price increases of 15-20% in the second to third quarters.
Passengers are booking later, but airlines continue to indicate a
strong propensity to ﬂy, especially in premium and premium-
economy classes. A minority also accounts for a large share of
ﬂights. In the Netherlands, for example, 25% of people account for

75% of journeys. These frequent travellers tend to be less price-
sensitive.
There has therefore been some demand destruction wiping out part
of the underlying growth. So far, though, the propensity to travel
has remained resilient.
Aviation remains proﬁtable, but margins hit
a post-pandemic low
The fuel shock has signiﬁcantly aﬀected margins. On average,
proﬁts in the global industry are expected to drop meaningfully
below 5%. Airlines including Ryanair, Southwest Airlines and AirAsia

have scaled back capacity on unproﬁtable routes as elevated fuel
prices persisted longer than expected.
According to global aviation consultancy Cirium, around 1% of
global air travel capacity will be removed heading into the Northern
Hemisphere winter of 2026-27. In less mature markets, maintaining
volumes while passing on the higher fuel bill is more diﬃcult.
Airlines may also prioritise less fuel-eﬃcient, older-generation
aircraft over capacity growth.

### Page 11

Industry headwinds could become a catalyst for further
consolidation, ranging from minority stakes to full takeovers.
Fares could retreat if fuel prices ease, but
don't expect too much
Dynamic and algorithm-based pricing is becoming increasingly
common. This helps airlines optimise already historically high load
factors (84% in 2026 so far) but can also contribute to greater fare
volatility, especially as passengers book later amid uncertainty.
If fuel prices ease in 2027, fares could drop back. Yet relatively

strong demand, constrained aircraft availability and general
inﬂation mean a return to early-2026 fare levels looks unlikely.
Cargo beneﬁts from high-tech demand and
supply-chain volatility
Air cargo continues to perform well as a fast transport option in an
increasingly volatile and less predictable world. Volume growth
remained almost 5% year-to-date, while freight rates remained
attractive for carriers.
While e-commerce drove much of the growth in recent years,

digitalisation and large-scale AI investment increasingly support
shipments of high-value technology products. Strong cargo
performance helped cushion pressure on airline results in 2026.
European carriers also beneﬁted from reduced available freight
capacity from Middle Eastern competitors.
Freighter capacity received a boost during the pandemic through
passenger-to-freighter conversions, but that boost has largely run
its course. New-generation A350 and Boeing 777 freighters should

add capacity in the coming years.
Aircargo volume resilient in disruptive
geopolitical environment

Future demand 2027 and onwards
Aviation’s centre of gravity is increasingly
shifting eastwards
The centre of gravity of global aviation is increasingly shifting
eastwards. Trips per capita are expected to see strong increases
over the next two decades, particularly in Asia. This matters even
more because these markets are far more populous.
Until recently, around 90% of the world’s population did not ﬂy in a
given year, but this is gradually changing. As a result, ﬂeet growth is

expected to be strongest in Asia, with its share of the global aircraft
ﬂeet increasing from around 33% to approximately 45%.
People still expected to ﬂy more, especially
in India

### Page 13

Source: S&P, Airbus, ING Research
India is becoming a powerhouse for future
airline growth
Air travel is strongly correlated with GDP and household income. As
Asia’s middle class expands, the potential customer base for air
travel swells. For future aviation demand, this income eﬀect is even
more important than population growth alone.
Younger generations also appear keen to travel, adding to the
potential in countries such as India and Indonesia. Increasingly

internationally dispersed families provide another structural source
of demand.
India stands out here. Strong economic growth, rising household
incomes and a huge population oﬀer strong potential. Airlines are
positioning themselves accordingly, illustrated by historically large
aircraft orders from IndiGo and Air India. India’s aircraft ﬂeet is
expected to grow by around 8% annually on average over the next
two decades.
China and India are the growth engines of

global aviation in the decades ahead

### Page 14

Source: S&P Global, Airbus, ING Research
Supply-side developments
Aircraft scarcity keeps capacity tight and
lease rates elevated
Supply constraints have been curbing aviation growth since the
post-pandemic recovery and remain a major factor. The share of
stored aircraft dropped below 10% by the end of 2025, its lowest
level since 2019. Even several A380s (the largest aircraft) have
returned to service.
More than half of the global commercial aircraft ﬂeet is leased.

Tight capacity is consequently reﬂected in relatively high used-
aircraft values and lease rates.
Airbus and Boeing had a combined record backlog of around
15,000 aircraft by mid-2026. Production and deliveries continue to
lag order intake after seven consecutive years of subdued deliveries
from 2019 onwards. Demand for new-generation aircraft remains
strong, as illustrated by major recent orders from Avolon and
Turkish Airlines.
Production could ﬁnally return to pre-pandemic levels in 2027.

Chinese manufacturer COMAC, meanwhile, is making progress in
China in Asia, but Western certiﬁcation remains distant.

### Page 15

New airplane deliveries continue on a
recovery track now with Boeing rebounding
Number of delivered commercial airplanes by manufacturers Airbus &

Source: Company reports *Company forecast
Why aircraft supply remains constrained
Production has still not caught up with pre-pandemic levels
and is unlikely to do so in the next ﬁve years. Aircraft
manufacturing is recovering, but delays and large backlogs
push delivery dates into the 2030s. Airlines are therefore forced

to keep older aircraft ﬂying for longer.
Suppliers are still struggling to ramp up. Six years after the
sharp pandemic downturn, aviation supply chains have still
not fully normalised. Stringent safety and certiﬁcation
requirements mean manufacturers rely on a limited number of
specialised suppliers. Engines and sophisticated cabin
components still have long lead times, while skilled workers
remain scarce.
Engine issues continue to aﬀect available capacity. The peak

impact of the Pratt & Whitney inspection programme after an
identiﬁed production issue has passed, but checks and
maintenance continue to constrain parts of the A320neo-
family ﬂeet and absorb scarce maintenance capacity.

Delivery catch-up is crucial for both
capacity and efﬁciency
New aircraft are critical not only to accommodate growth, but also
to improve eﬃciency. The average age of the global ﬂeet has
increased from around 13 to 15 years since 2019 due to production
constraints.
This matters increasingly as older units require more maintenance

at a time when skilled personnel and maintenance capacity are
scarce; they also consume signiﬁcantly more fuel and therefore
emit more.
Boeing is ﬁnally regaining altitude
supporting deliveries
Boeing is ﬁnally making a sustained comeback following an
unprecedented period of disruption stemming from the 737 MAX
accidents, the pandemic and subsequent production and quality
problems. It received certiﬁcation for its 737 MAX 7 and also the
737 MAX 10 recently. Boeing plans to increase production for the

family from 38 to 47 a month.

Higher 737 production should support deliveries in 2027 and
gradually bring pre-pandemic production levels back into sight. This
is hugely important for Boeing-dependent airlines such as Ryanair
and Southwest. Progress on certiﬁcation of the 777-9 is also
important for long-haul ﬂeet renewal.
Fleet renewal is currently the most
important decarbonisation lever
Air travel continues to grow, and associated emissions tend to
continue to rise further as well. Sustainable aviation fuels are

labelled as an important industry pathway to decarbonise, but
globally, blend rates still hover below 1% in 2026.
The closure of airspaces, too, has created ineﬃciencies and extra
emissions over the last few years, undermining gains elsewhere.
Around 63% of the global ﬂeet still consists of previous-generation
aircraft. Replacing them can reduce fuel consumption by 10-25% or
more, making ﬂeet renewal one of the most eﬀective near-term
decarbonisation levers available to airlines.

The challenge is to deliver proﬁtable growth

deliver proﬁtable growth in a world of persistently constrained
capacity, higher costs and more frequent disruption.

## Document 325: GF - PTFE Adoption

Source PDF: all report pdf/GF - PTFE Adoption.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-18
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Equity Research 
Sector Report | Overseas Technology｜Oct 15,2026 
PTFE Adoption: 
Reshape the High-End Materials Landscape

Overseas  
Technology

Michelle Jing 
SFC CE No. BUK594 
michellejing@gfgroup.com.hk 
Jeff Pu, CFA 
SFC CE No. BNO719 
jeffpu@gfgroup.com.hk

What's new 
Nvidia showcased its NVLink PTFE board prototype for the first time at OCP Global Summit 2026, highlighting seven supply-chain partners: 
Taiflex (8039 TT), Flexium (6269 TT), TTM Technologies (TTMI US), 3 leading Chinese PCB manufacturers, and 1 leading Chinese CCL 
supplier. We believe this development reinforces our previous view that PTFE remains the most promising material solution for the VR300 
NVL576 switch board, offering the best balance between electrical performance and MP feasibility. 
Comments 
PTFE hybrid remains the preferred solution: As highlighted in our Oct. 5 report, we continue to favor the PTFE hybrid solution for the 
VR300 NVL576 switch board among the various options, given its optimal balance between electrical performance and MP feasibility. 
While pure PTFE remains challenging to process, particularly in lamination and drilling, leading PCB manufacturers have achie ved 
encouraging yields with hybrid structures that combine PTFE signal layers with M9 materials in non-signal layers. 
We like PTFE's long-term adoption potential: PTFE applications in high-frequency and high-speed interconnects could extend beyond 
switch boards. The IEC has published a standard on PTFE-filled CCL, led by the leading Chinese CCL company, highlighting the material's 
relevance to broader circuit-board applications. Our checks also suggest that PTFE materials have entered the testing phase for other 
applications, including compute boards. We therefore remain positive on PTFE's long-term adoption across a broader range of high-end 
PCB applications. 
Limited near-term impact on conventional resin materials, but long-term substitution risks warrant attention: Under the current 
PTFE hybrid design, we expect 8–12 out of 32 layers to adopt PTFE for signal transmission, accounting for less than 50% of the total CCL 
resin content value. Over the longer term, we expect a potential shift toward pure PTFE solutions as improvements in processing 
technology enable commercially viable MP yields, reducing the need for hybrid structures. This could gradually increase substitution risks 
for conventional high-end PCB resin materials, including PPO and HC resin. 
Favor PTFE-exposed suppliers with proven technical and manufacturing capabilities: We remain positive on PTFE-related 
opportunities, particularly suppliers with strong R&D capabilities, proven MP expertise, and established customer qualifications. In CCL, 
we favor Taiflex (8039 TT) and leading Chinese CCL supplier for their PTFE material capabilities and supply-chain exposure. Among the 
five Nvidia-announced PCB suppliers, we favor the leading Chinese PTFE PCB supplier, given its advanced hybrid PTFE processing 
capabilities and encouraging yields. For CCL suppliers that have yet to enter the PTFE supply chain, we recommend monitoring progress 
in developing, while remaining alert to potential market-share losses in their conventional product portfolios. 
Risks  
1) AI demand slowing down; 2) Geo-political risks; 3) Competition.

## Document 357: MSCI、トルコ株式指数の変更を凍結し、格下げの可能性を警告 ～トルコ株式市場の混乱は長期化の様相、MSCIは改革の進捗を注視～

Source PDF: all report pdf/MSCI、トルコ株式指数の変更を凍結し、格下げの可能性を警告 ～トルコ株式市場の混乱は長期化の様相、MSCIは改革の進捗を注視～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-16
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Asia Trends

2026 年10 月 16 日

1/3 
MSCI、トルコ株式指数の変更を凍結し、格下げの可能性を警告 
～トルコ株式市場の混乱は長期化の様相、MSCI は改革の進捗を注視～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ トルコ株式市場では、 投資信託による株式売却圧力を背景に下落基調が続いている。MSCI は
６月、一部投資信託による低流動性銘柄への保有集中や浮動株比率の歪みに懸念を示し、当
局に情報開示や市場監視の強化を求めた。背景には、政府が国内資金をリラ建て資産に誘導
したことで、低浮動株企業の上場や投資信託への資金流入が拡大し、低流動性銘柄の株価上
昇と資金流入が相互に増幅する構造が形成されたことがある。 
○ その後、資本市場委員会（ＳＰＫ）が投資信託の保有上限を厳格化し、年末までに超過分の
解消を求めたことで資金循環が逆回転した。株価下落に伴う解約増加から、一部運用会社が
解約請求に応じられない事態となり、現金確保を目的に流動性の高い銘柄にも売りが波及し
た。 当局は資金供給の拡充や投資信託の取引停止・清算のほか、 関係者を刑事告発するなど、
早期の事態収拾に向けた取り組みを強化している。 
○ MSCI は10 月15 日、 トルコ株式指数の変更を凍結し、2027 年６月までに十分な市場改革が進
まなければ、新興国市場からフロンティア市場などへの格下げを検討する可能性を示した。
格下げとなればパッシブ投資家を中心に資金流出が加速する懸念がある。加えて、利下げ観
測に伴うリラ安から家計資金が外貨や金に流れる可能性もあり、市場混乱の長期化が実体経
済や政府の政策運営に波及するリスクに注意が必要である。

トルコ株の下落基調が続いている（図１）（注１）。背景には、現金確保を目的に投資信託が株式
の売却圧力を強めていることがある。今年６月、指数算出会社である MSCI が一部の投資信託によ
る株式保有の集中や、流動性の低い銘柄に関する警告を発したことがきっかけとなった。 
具体的には、特定の中小上場企業と密接な関係にある投資信託の保有分が、浮動株比率の推計値
を人為的に押し上げていると、海外の機関投資家から懸念が寄せられていることを明らかにした。
こうした懸念は、当該銘柄の指数構成比率を歪めることにつながる。

注１ 10 月９日付レポート「トルコ金融市場の混乱が収まらない」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/666443.html）

### Page 2

2/3 
このため、同社は当局に対して、実質
的な所有者に関する詳細かつ適時の情
報開示のほか、 協調的な取引行動に対す
る強固な監視と取り締まり、 構造的に歪
められた浮動株を特定するための透明
性が高いルールに基づく枠組みの構築
を求める考えを示した。そのうえで、11
月に実施する指数見直しまでに十分か
つ具体的な進展が見られない場合、 同国
と対象銘柄の適切な取り扱いについて
協議を開始する可能性があるとした。 
こうした懸念が高まった背景には、2023 年に財務相に返り咲いたシムシェキ氏の下、 同国政府が
国内貯蓄をリラ建て資産にとどめる取り組みを進めたことがある。そのため、2021 年に導入された
為替保護型預金（ＫＫＭ：リラ安による為替下落率が預金金利を上回った場合、その差額を政府・
中銀が補填する制度）を廃止し、通常のリラ建て預金への移行を促進したほか、株式や投資信託な
どにも資金を誘導した。 
一方で、同国では高金利状態が長期化しており、政府は企業に借入ではなく、株式市場の活用に
よる資金調達の積極化を推進したため、浮動株比率が低い企業が数多く上場した。こうした銘柄は
市場で売買可能な株式数が限られ、株価が大きく変動しやすいことで、価格形成に歪みが生じた。
さらに、 その後の投資ブームを追い風に投資信託が急拡大し、 投資家にとっては株式投資の間口が
広がる一方、リスクの集中が懸念された。具体的には、投資信託に組み入れられる比較的流動性が
低い銘柄への資金流入が活発化し、 株価上昇に伴い運用収益が向上した結果、 新たな資金流入を生
む循環が形成された。 
規制機関である資本市場委員会（ＳＰＫ）は８月、投資信託の純資産に対する組入比率、発行済
株式数に対する保有割合など保有ポジションの上限を厳格化し、2026 年末までに超過分の解消を
義務付ける規制変更を発表した。具体的には、10 月末までに超過分の３分の１、11 月末までに３
分の２、12 月末までに完全解消を求めている。 
その結果、 株価を押し上げてきた資金流入の循環が逆回転し、 株価下落により投資信託の解約請
求が膨らんだ。 さらに、 複数の資産運用会社が投資家による解約請求に応じられないと発表する事
態に発展した。 流動性の低い保有資産の売却が困難になり、 現金確保を目的に流動性の高い銘柄に
売却圧力が強まったことで、市場全体に売り圧力が広がっている。 
市場の混乱を受けて、 中銀は流動性確保を目的とするレポ資金の供給額引き上げのほか、 銀行間
資金市場における銀行の借入限度額も大幅に引き上げた。ＳＰＫも、複数の投資信託の取引停止・
清算を命じたほか、関係者を刑事告発するといった動きをみせている。
